		private void cmdFind_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("varyno"));
			if (text.Length > 1 && int.TryParse(text.Substring(1), out var result))
			{
				result++;
				MmgExtensions.GxSearchVariable((MMViewModel)(object)this, ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main"), result, "", "", "VARY");
				Display2();
				((MMViewModel)this).UpdateDisplay((object)null);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnUpdateData()
		{
			if (((CxvViewModel)this).IsInitialUpdate)
			{
				Collection<string> oplist = CxsConverter.ValueToStringCollection(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", "option_list"));
				_varTypes = VarAccessing.VAVarTypeInit(oplist);
				_prompts = VarAccessing.VAVaryPromptInit();
				Display2();
			}
			((MMTabPageViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
		}

		protected override void OnSubPropertyDataChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration pd, string subPropertyName)
		{
			((CxvViewModel)this).OnSubPropertyDataChanged(pd, subPropertyName);
			if ((pd.Name == "MMFortranLine_Incr" && subPropertyName == "Value") || (pd.Name == "MMFortranLine_StartPoint" && subPropertyName == "Value") || (pd.Name == "MMFortranLine_EndPoint" && subPropertyName == "Value") || (pd.Name == "MMOptionGroup1_MultiMap" && subPropertyName == "Value") || (pd.Name == "MMOptionGroup2_MultiMap" && subPropertyName == "Value"))
			{
				string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main.CC Nodes.INTPT.@varyno");
				int? num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.BatchGet(text, "value", (string)null));
				if (num.HasValue && num.Value == 0)
				{
					WarningImageVisibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				else
				{
					WarningImageVisibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
			}
			else if (subPropertyName == "Value")
			{
				Display2();
			}
		}

		protected override void OnViewChanged()
		{
			_varyView = ((MMViewModel)this).View as MMSensitivi_Input_Vary;
			Display2();
			((MMViewModel)this).OnViewChanged();
		}

		private int IsMultiCaseConsole()
		{
			string name = WindowsIdentity.GetCurrent().Name;
			NamedPipeClientStream namedPipeClientStream = new NamedPipeClientStream(".", name, PipeDirection.InOut);
			try
			{
				namedPipeClientStream.Connect(1000);
				if (namedPipeClientStream.IsConnected)
				{
					namedPipeClientStream.ReadMode = PipeTransmissionMode.Message;
					string s = new StreamReader(namedPipeClientStream).ReadLine();
					namedPipeClientStream.Close();
					if (int.TryParse(s, out var result))
					{
						return result;
					}
					return 0;
				}
			}
			catch
			{
				return 0;
			}
			return 0;
		}

		private string GetMultiCaseUrl()
		{
			string multicase_urlroot = ConfigurationManager.AppSettings["MultiCaseAddress"];
			if (!IsMultiCaseRunning(ref multicase_urlroot) && StartMultiCaseConsole() && !IsMultiCaseRunning(ref multicase_urlroot))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return multicase_urlroot;
		}

		private bool IsMultiCaseRunning(ref string multicase_urlroot)
		{
			int num = 0;
			if ((num = IsMultiCaseConsole()) != 0)
			{
				UriBuilder uriBuilder = new UriBuilder(new Uri(multicase_urlroot));
				uriBuilder.Port = num;
				multicase_urlroot = uriBuilder.Uri.AbsoluteUri;
			}
			bool result = true;
			WebRequest webRequest = WebRequest.Create(multicase_urlroot + "api/MultiCaseProjectAction/GetStatus");
			try
			{
				using HttpWebResponse httpWebResponse = (HttpWebResponse)webRequest.GetResponse();
				httpWebResponse.Close();
				if (httpWebResponse.StatusCode != HttpStatusCode.OK)
				{
					result = false;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				result = false;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private bool StartMultiCaseConsole()
		{
			try
			{
				string text = default(string);
				string text2 = default(string);
				string text3 = default(string);
				string text4 = default(string);
				string text5 = default(string);
				string text6 = default(string);
				((MMViewModel)this).GetProductInfo(ref text, ref text2, ref text3, ref text4, ref text5, ref text6);
				string text7 = text2.Split('.')[0];
				string text8 = RegistryKey.OpenBaseKey(RegistryHive.LocalMachine, RegistryView.Default).OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\WOW6432Node\\AspenTech\\Setup\\Products\\MULTICASE" + text7 + "0\\Destinations").GetValue("CONSOLE") as string;
				return new Process
				{
					StartInfo = new ProcessStartInfo
					{
						FileName = Path.Combine(text8, "AspenTech.MultiCase.Console.exe"),
						WorkingDirectory = text8,
						Arguments = "-nobrowser"
					}
				}.Start();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}

		private void cmdSendToMulticase_Execute(object parameter)
		{
			//IL_00a5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00aa: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00c0: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00cc: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00d8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ea: Expected O, but got Unknown
			MultiCaseExportConfiguration multiCaseExportConfiguration = new MultiCaseExportConfiguration();
			multiCaseExportConfiguration.ShowDialog();
			if ((!multiCaseExportConfiguration.DialogResult).Value)
			{
				return;
			}
			MultiCaseExportConfiguration.ScenarioType exportType = multiCaseExportConfiguration.ExportType;
			bool exportMethod = multiCaseExportConfiguration.ExportMethod;
			CxsCase val = UnityContainerExtensions.Resolve<CxsCase>(((CxvViewModel)this).UnityContainer, Array.Empty<ResolverOverride>());
			if (!CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", "has_children")).Value || !CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE", "has_children")).Value)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ShowMMMessageBox(new ShowMMMessageBoxEventArgs
				{
					Message = ApwnResources.InputIncomplete,
					Title = ApwnResources.InputIncomplete,
					MsgBoxButton = 0,
					DialogResult = 1,
					MsgBoxImage = 16
				});
				return;
			}
			string text = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.Personal), "AspenTech\\Aspen Multicase\\" + Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(val.CaseFileName));
			string text2 = Path.Combine(text, ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main").Substring(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main").LastIndexOf(".") + 1) + ".json");
			if (!Directory.Exists(text))
			{
				Directory.CreateDirectory(text);
			}
			SecurityIdentifier identity = new SecurityIdentifier(WellKnownSidType.LocalServiceSid, null);
			DirectoryInfo directoryInfo = new DirectoryInfo(text);
			DirectorySecurity accessControl = directoryInfo.GetAccessControl();
			FileSystemAccessRule rule = new FileSystemAccessRule(identity, FileSystemRights.FullControl, InheritanceFlags.ContainerInherit | InheritanceFlags.ObjectInherit, PropagationFlags.NoPropagateInherit, AccessControlType.Allow);
			accessControl.AddAccessRule(rule);
			directoryInfo.SetAccessControl(accessControl);
			string text3 = Path.Combine(text, val.CaseFileName);
			File.Copy(val.CaseFullFilePath, text3, overwrite: true);
			string text4 = createMultiCaseJsonContents();
			if (!exportMethod)
			{
				File.WriteAllText(text2, text4);
				string multiCaseUrl = GetMultiCaseUrl();
				string[] obj = new string[7]
				{
					multiCaseUrl,
					"app/multicase/createProject/:",
					Uri.EscapeDataString(text3),
					"?type=",
					null,
					null,
					null
				};
				int num = (int)exportType;
				obj[4] = num.ToString();
				obj[5] = "&jsonPath=";
				obj[6] = Uri.EscapeDataString(text2);
				multiCaseUrl = string.Concat(obj);
				if (exportType == MultiCaseExportConfiguration.ScenarioType.Unified && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(multiCaseExportConfiguration.UnifiedJsonFilePath))
				{
					string text5 = Path.Combine(text, Path.GetFileName(multiCaseExportConfiguration.UnifiedJsonFilePath));
					File.Copy(multiCaseExportConfiguration.UnifiedJsonFilePath, text5, overwrite: true);
					multiCaseUrl = multiCaseUrl + "&sceondjson=" + Uri.EscapeDataString(text5);
				}
				try
				{
					Process.Start(multiCaseUrl);
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			MultiCaseSaveJsonToFile(text4);
		}

		private void MultiCaseSaveJsonToFile(string jsonContent)
		{
			System.Windows.Forms.SaveFileDialog saveFileDialog = new System.Windows.Forms.SaveFileDialog();
			saveFileDialog.Filter = "JSON files (*.json)|*.json";
			saveFileDialog.FilterIndex = 2;
			if (saveFileDialog.ShowDialog() != DialogResult.Cancel)
			{
				File.WriteAllText((Path.GetExtension(saveFileDialog.FileName) == ".json") ? saveFileDialog.FileName : (saveFileDialog.FileName + ".json"), jsonContent);
			}
		}

		private string createMultiCaseJsonContents()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
			string[] array = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", "child_ids"));
			bool flag = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.CASES", "value")) == "YES";
			string[] array2 = array;
			foreach (string text in array2)
			{
				string cXSPath = GetCXSPath(text, _prompts, isDependent: false);
				string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARYUOM." + text, "value"));
				string text3 = "{ \"Unit\" : \"" + text2 + "\",";
				string text4 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.OPT_LIST." + text, "value"));
				if ((text4 == "RANGE" || text4 == "LOGARITHMIC") && !flag)
				{
					text3 = text3 + "\"VarLowBound\" : \"" + CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.LOWER." + text, "value")) + "\",";
					text3 = text3 + "\"VarHighBound\" : \"" + CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.UPPER." + text, "value")) + "\",";
					if (text4 == "RANGE")
					{
						text3 += "\"VarScaleType\" : \"Linear\",";
						string text5 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.OPT_RANGE." + text, "value"));
						double? num = CxsConverter.ValueToDouble(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + text5 + "." + text, "value"));
						if (num.HasValue && text5 == "INCR")
						{
							text3 = text3 + "\"VarInterval\" : \"" + num + "\",";
						}
						if (num.HasValue && text5 == "NPOINT")
						{
							text3 = text3 + "\"VarStepSize\" : \"" + num + "\",";
						}
					}
					else
					{
						text3 += "\"VarScaleType\" : \"Logarithmic\",";
						text3 = text3 + "\"VarStepSize\" : \"" + CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.NPOINT." + text, "value")) + "\",";
						text3 = text3 + "\"VarFactor\" : \"" + CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FACTOR." + text, "value")) + "\",";
					}
				}
				else if (flag)
				{
					List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
					string[] array3 = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VALUES", "child_ids"));
					foreach (string text6 in array3)
					{
						list3.Add(CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VALUES." + text6 + "." + text, "value")));
					}
					text3 = text3 + "\"States\" : \"" + string.Join(",", list3) + "\",";
				}
				text3 = text3 + "\"Identifier\" : \"" + cXSPath + "\",";
				text3 += "\"Tag\" : \"\" }";
				if (flag)
				{
					list2.Add(text3);
				}
				else
				{
					list.Add(text3);
				}
			}
			array2 = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE", "child_ids"));
			foreach (string text7 in array2)
			{
				string text8 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE." + text7, "value", (string)null, watch)).Replace("-", "");
				string cXSPath2 = GetCXSPath(text7, VarAccessing.VAPromptInit(), isDependent: true);
				string text9 = "{ \"Unit\" : \"" + CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(cXSPath2, "uom")) + "\",";
				text9 = ((!BasisStreamProps.ContainsKey(text8)) ? (text9 + "\"Identifier\" : \"" + cXSPath2 + "\",") : (text9 + "\"Identifier\" : \"" + cXSPath2.Replace(text8, BasisStreamProps[text8]) + "\","));
				text9 += "\"Tag\" : \"\" }";
				list.Add(text9);
			}
			string text10 = "{ \"Name\" : \"" + ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main").Substring(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main").LastIndexOf(".") + 1) + "\",";
			if (flag)
			{
				text10 = text10 + "\"NStates\": \"" + CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VALUES", "child_ids")).Length + "\",";
				text10 += "\"Type\": \"1\",";
			}
			text10 += "\"Variables\" : [";
			text10 += string.Join(",", list);
			if (list2.Count > 0)
			{
				text10 += "],\"DiscreteVariables\" : [";
				text10 += string.Join(",", list2);
			}
			return text10 + "]}";
		}

		public string GetCXSPath(string strValue, List<PromptType> lPrompts, bool isDependent)
		{
			string result = "";
			string text = null;
			string text2 = null;
			string text3 = null;
			string text4 = null;
			string text5 = null;
			string text6 = null;
			string text7 = null;
			string text8 = null;
			string text9 = null;
			string text10 = null;
			string text11 = null;
			string text12 = null;
			string text13 = (isDependent ? "Output." : "Input.");
			string text14 = null;
			text14 = (isDependent ? CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE." + strValue, "value", (string)null, watch)) : CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE." + strValue, "value", (string)null, watch)));
			if (text14 == null)
			{
				return result;
			}
			string text15 = "";
			text15 = text14.Replace("MOLE-", string.Empty).Replace("STDVOL-", string.Empty).Replace("MASS-", string.Empty)
				.Replace("VOL-", string.Empty);
			string text16 = "Top.appModelV8.";
			foreach (PromptType lPrompt in lPrompts)
			{
				if (lPrompt.Variable == "VARYBLOCK" || lPrompt.Variable == "FVN_BLOCK")
				{
					text5 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + lPrompt.Variable + "." + strValue, "value", (string)null, watch));
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5))
					{
						if (text5.Contains("."))
						{
							string[] array = text5.Split('.');
							for (int i = 0; i < array.Count(); i++)
							{
								text16 = ((i != array.Count() - 1) ? (text16 + "Blocks." + array[i] + ".Data.") : (text16 + "Blocks." + array[i]));
							}
							text12 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(text16, "type"));
							text16 = text16 + "." + text13;
						}
						else
						{
							text16 = text16 + "Blocks." + text5;
							text12 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(text16, "type"));
							text16 = text16 + "." + text13;
						}
					}
				}
				if (lPrompt.Variable == "VARYSTREAM" || lPrompt.Variable == "FVN_STREAM")
				{
					text4 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + lPrompt.Variable + "." + strValue, "value", (string)null, watch));
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
					{
						if (text4.Contains("."))
						{
							string[] array2 = text4.Split('.');
							for (int j = 0; j < array2.Count(); j++)
							{
								text16 = ((j != array2.Count() - 1) ? (text16 + "Streams." + array2[j] + ".Data.") : (text16 + "Streams." + array2[j]));
							}
							text16 = text16 + "." + text13;
						}
						else
						{
							text16 = text16 + "Streams." + text4 + "." + text13;
						}
					}
				}
				if (lPrompt.Variable == "VARYSUBS" || lPrompt.Variable == "FVN_SUBS")
				{
					text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + lPrompt.Variable + "." + strValue, "value", (string)null, watch));
				}
				if (lPrompt.Variable == "VARYCOMPONEN" || lPrompt.Variable == "FVN_COMPONEN")
				{
					text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + lPrompt.Variable + "." + strValue, "value", (string)null, watch));
				}
				if (lPrompt.Variable == "VARYVARIABLE" || lPrompt.Variable == "FVN_VARIABLE")
				{
					text3 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + lPrompt.Variable + "." + strValue, "value", (string)null, watch));
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
					{
						string[] array3 = new string[3] { "MOLE", "MASS", "STDVOL" };
						foreach (string text17 in array3)
						{
							foreach (string ss in varBasisConversion.Values)
							{
								if (text17 + ss == text3)
								{
									text3 = varBasisConversion.FirstOrDefault((KeyValuePair<string, string> x) => x.Value == ss).Key;
								}
							}
						}
						if (text12 != null && text12 == "MHeatX" && (text3.StartsWith("COLD-") || text3.StartsWith("HOT-")))
						{
							text3 = "VALUE";
						}
						text3 = text3.Replace("-", "_");
						if (text3 == "FLOW/FRAC")
						{
							text3 = "FRAC";
						}
					}
				}
				if (lPrompt.Variable == "VARYID1" || lPrompt.Variable == "FVN_ID1")
				{
					text6 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + lPrompt.Variable + "." + strValue, "value", (string)null, watch));
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text6) && !isDependent)
					{
						text16 = text16 + (text16.EndsWith(".") ? "" : ".") + text3 + "." + text6;
					}
				}
				if (lPrompt.Variable == "VARYID2" || lPrompt.Variable == "FVN_ID2")
				{
					text7 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + lPrompt.Variable + "." + strValue, "value", (string)null, watch));
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text7) && !isDependent)
					{
						text16 = text16 + "." + text7;
					}
				}
				if (lPrompt.Variable == "VARYID3" || lPrompt.Variable == "FVN_ID3")
				{
					text8 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + lPrompt.Variable + "." + strValue, "value", (string)null, watch));
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text8) && !isDependent)
					{
						text16 = text16 + "." + text8;
					}
				}
				if (lPrompt.Variable == "FVN_ATTRIB")
				{
					text9 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + lPrompt.Variable + "." + strValue, "value", (string)null, watch));
				}
				if (lPrompt.Variable == "FVN_ELEM")
				{
					text10 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + lPrompt.Variable + "." + strValue, "value", (string)null, watch));
				}
				if (lPrompt.Variable == "FVN_SENTENCE")
				{
					text11 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + lPrompt.Variable + "." + strValue, "value", (string)null, watch));
				}
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
			{
				if (text15 == "FLOW")
				{
					text16 = ((!(text14 == "MASS-FLOW" && isDependent)) ? (text16 + text15 + "." + text + "." + text2) : (text16 + "Substreams." + text + ".MassComponentFlows." + text2));
				}
				else
				{
					text3 += text14.Replace("-", "");
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text9) && text3 == "COMPATTRVAR")
						{
							if (isDependent)
							{
								string text18 = text16 + ".STR_CATT.COMP-ATTR." + text + "." + text2 + "." + text9;
								string[] array4 = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(text18, "child_ids"));
								int result2 = 0;
								if (array4.Any() && array4.Length > 1 && int.TryParse(text10, out result2))
								{
									string text19 = array4[result2 - 1];
									text16 = text16 + ".ComponentAttributes." + text + "." + text2 + "." + text9 + "." + text19;
								}
								else
								{
									text16 = text16 + ".ComponentAttributes." + text + "." + text2 + "." + text9;
								}
							}
							else
							{
								text16 = text16 + "." + text9;
								if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text10))
								{
									text16 = text16 + "." + text10;
								}
							}
						}
						else
						{
							text16 = text16 + "Substreams." + text + "." + text3 + "." + text2;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				if (!isDependent)
				{
					switch (text3)
					{
					case "MOLE_FLOW":
					case "MASS_FLOW":
					case "STDVOL_FLOW":
						text3 = "TOTFLOW";
						break;
					}
					text16 = text16 + text3 + "." + text;
				}
				else if (BasisStreamProps.ContainsKey(text3.Replace("_", "")))
				{
					text16 = text16 + "Substreams." + text + "." + BasisStreamProps[text3.Replace("_", "")];
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						text16 = text16.Replace("Component", "").Replace("Flows", "Flow");
					}
				}
				else
				{
					text16 = text16 + text3 + "." + text;
				}
			}
			else if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text6) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(text7) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(text8))
			{
				if (text3.StartsWith("CALC_"))
				{
					text3 = text3.Replace("CALC_", "");
				}
				text16 += text3;
			}
			else if (text14 == "BLOCK-VAR" && isDependent)
			{
				text3 = (OutputVarConvert.ContainsKey(text3) ? OutputVarConvert[text3] : text3);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text11) && text11 == "CA-SEC-RES")
				{
					text16 = text16 + "CAOptionResult." + text6 + ".CASectionResult." + text7 + "." + text3;
				}
				else
				{
					text16 += text3;
					text16 += ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text6)) ? ("." + text6) : "");
					text16 += ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text7)) ? ("." + text7) : "");
					text16 += ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text8)) ? ("." + text8) : "");
				}
			}
			return ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath(text16);
		}

		private void Display2()
		{
			if (_bExecutingDisplay2)
			{
				return;
			}
			_bExecutingDisplay2 = true;
			List<PromptType> list = VarAccessing.VAVaryPromptInit();
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("varyno"));
			object obj = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE." + text, "value");
			string text2 = obj as string;
			int num = 0;
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_BlockVarVisible1", (object)"0", true);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_HybridVarVisible", (object)"0", true);
			for (int i = 0; i < cmdFind_Visibility.Count; i++)
			{
				cmdFind_Visibility[i] = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < list.Count; j++)
				{
					string text3 = "@main.Input." + list[j].Variable + "." + text;
					if (((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider == null || ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.Case == null || ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.Case.ResolveNode(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath(text3)) == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(text3, "can_enter"));
					if ((!flag.HasValue || !flag.Value) && (string.Compare(list[j].Text, Resources.PromptType_Text_Units, StringComparison.Ordinal) != 0 || string.IsNullOrEmpty(CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(text3, "value")))))
					{
						continue;
					}
					lblrows_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Visible;
					lblrows_Caption[num] = list[j].Text;
					if (num == 4)
					{
						lblrows_Caption_4 = list[j].Text;
					}
					object obj2 = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(text3, "option_list");
					if (obj2 == null || obj2.ToString() == string.Empty || obj2.GetType() == typeof(DBNull) || list[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Sentence)
					{
						cboRows_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Collapsed;
						txtRows_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else
					{
						cboRows_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Visible;
						txtRows_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
					if (list[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Variable && cboRows_Visibility[num] == Visibility.Visible)
					{
						cmdFind_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Visible;
						ComboWidth[num] = 142;
					}
					else if (list[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Variable && (text2 == "BLOCK-VAR" || text2 == "SENSOR-VAR"))
					{
						cboRows_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Collapsed;
						txtRows_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Collapsed;
						string text4 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARYBLOCK.@varyno", "value"));
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
						{
							text4 = ((MMViewModel)this).GetFullBlockName("@main", text4);
							if (((MMViewModel)this).GetModelCategoryByBlockName(text4) == 14 || text2 == "SENSOR-VAR")
							{
								((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_HybridVarVisible", (object)"1", true);
							}
							else
							{
								((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_BlockVarVisible1", (object)"1", true);
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						ComboWidth[num] = 170;
					}
					if (((MMViewModel)this).View is MMSensitivi_Input_Vary mMSensitivi_Input_Vary)
					{
						object obj3 = ((FrameworkElement)(object)mMSensitivi_Input_Vary).FindName("txtRows_" + num);
						MMTextEdit val = (MMTextEdit)((obj3 is MMTextEdit) ? obj3 : null);
						object obj4 = ((FrameworkElement)(object)mMSensitivi_Input_Vary).FindName("cboRows_" + num);
						MMComboEdit val2 = (MMComboEdit)((obj4 is MMComboEdit) ? obj4 : null);
						if (!flag.HasValue || !flag.Value || list[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Sentence)
						{
							if (val != null)
							{
								((AesControlBase)val).IsMMEnabled = false;
							}
							if (val2 != null)
							{
								((AesControlBase)val2).IsMMEnabled = false;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							if (val != null)
							{
								((AesControlBase)val).IsMMEnabled = true;
							}
							if (val2 != null)
							{
								((AesControlBase)val2).IsMMEnabled = true;
							}
						}
						if (val2 != null)
						{
							if ((list[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Units || list[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Sentence) && !((AesControlBase)val2).IsMMEnabled)
							{
								((AesComboEdit)val2).DisplayAsTextBox = true;
							}
							else
							{
								((AesComboEdit)val2).DisplayAsTextBox = false;
							}
						}
					}
					if (list[j].ConvertToMixedCase)
					{
						((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("Rowsvw_" + num + "_IsMixedCase", (object)"1");
					}
					else
					{
						((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("Rowsvw_" + num + "_IsMixedCase", (object)"0");
					}
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("row" + num.ToString(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture), (object)list[j].Variable);
					num++;
					if (num > 7)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
				if (obj as string == "" || obj == DBNull.Value)
				{
					num = 0;
				}
			}
			for (int k = num; k <= 7; k++)
			{
				lblrows_Visibility[k] = Visibility.Collapsed;
				cboRows_Visibility[k] = Visibility.Collapsed;
				txtRows_Visibility[k] = Visibility.Collapsed;
				cmdFind_Visibility[k] = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			_bExecutingDisplay2 = false;
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("ComboWidth");
		}

		private CxvVariableData GetData(int index)
		{
			object obj = null;
			switch (index)
			{
			case 0:
				obj = txtRows_0_var1;
				break;
			case 1:
				obj = txtRows_1_var1;
				break;
			case 2:
				obj = txtRows_2_var1;
				break;
			case 3:
				obj = txtRows_3_var1;
				break;
			case 4:
				obj = txtRows_4_var1;
				break;
			case 5:
				obj = txtRows_5_var1;
				break;
			case 6:
				obj = txtRows_6_var1;
				break;
			case 7:
				obj = txtRows_7_var1;
				break;
			}
			return (CxvVariableData)((obj is CxvVariableData) ? obj : null);
		}

		private bool CanMove(bool up)
		{
			return MultipleMovingHelper.CanMove(GetRowCount(), (IEnumerable<int>)SelectedRowIndexes, up, true);
		}

		private void MoveSelection(bool up)
		{
			if (CanMove(up))
			{
				int rowCount = GetRowCount();
				IDictionary<int, int> movingTargets = MultipleMovingHelper.GetMovingTargets(rowCount, (IEnumerable<int>)SelectedRowIndexes, up, true);
				IList<int> newIndexesAfterMoved = MultipleMovingHelper.GetNewIndexesAfterMoved(rowCount, movingTargets, true);
				((MMViewModel)this).ReorderChildren("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", (IEnumerable<int>)newIndexesAfterMoved);
				SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int>(movingTargets.Values);
				RefreshButtonStatus();
			}
		}

		public override bool PasteVariable(IEnumerable<string> paths)
		{
			if (paths != null && paths.Count() > 0)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				string[] oldIds = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", "child_ids"));
				foreach (string path in paths)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
					{
						SmgeExtensions.SMVariableAccessingParse((MMViewModel)(object)this, path, (VarAcessType)1, ref flag);
					}
				}
				UpdateSelectedRow(oldIds);
				return true;
			}
			return ((MMViewModel)this).PasteVariable(paths);
		}

		public MMSensitivi_Input_Vary()
		{
			((MMViewModelContainer)this).ViewModelType = typeof(MMSensitivi_Input_Vary_ViewModel);
			InitializeComponent();
		}

		protected override bool IsAllowedPasteTarget(System.Windows.DragEventArgs e)
		{
			bool flag = ((MMTabPagePastable)this).IsAllowedPasteTarget(e);
			if (!flag)
			{
				DependencyObject ancestorFromType = Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject, typeof(MMScrollAreaMS), false);
				MMScrollAreaMS val = (MMScrollAreaMS)(object)((ancestorFromType is MMScrollAreaMS) ? ancestorFromType : null);
				flag = val != null && string.Equals(((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Name, "grdVary");
			}
			return flag;
		}

		public override bool OnRibbonCopy()
		{
			if (((MMViewModelContainer)this).ViewModel != null)
			{
				(((MMViewModelContainer)this).ViewModel as MMSensitivi_Input_Vary_ViewModel).cmdCopy_Command.Execute((object)null);
				return true;
			}
			return ((MMTabPagePastable)this).OnRibbonCopy();
		}

		public override bool OnRibbonCanPaste()
		{
			if (((MMViewModelContainer)this).ViewModel != null)
			{
				return (((MMViewModelContainer)this).ViewModel as MMSensitivi_Input_Vary_ViewModel).cmdPaste_Command.CanExecute((object)null);
			}
			return ((MMTabPagePastable)this).OnRibbonCanPaste();
		}

		public override bool OnRibbonPaste()
		{
			if (((MMViewModelContainer)this).ViewModel != null)
			{

		private void UpdateOptionId()
		{
			string text = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main.@parent.@parent.@parent");

		public void updateADSSensitivityResultsTableMapping()
		{
			//IL_0034: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0130: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0135: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_013c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_014a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0155: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0160: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_016d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_017f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0184: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_018b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0199: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01a4: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01af: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01bc: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_01c5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01cf: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_01e1: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01e6: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01ed: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01fb: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0206: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0211: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_021e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0238: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_023d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0244: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0254: Expected O, but got Unknown
			string obj = (string)((MMViewModelContainer)this).ViewModel.SharedParameters.Parameters.Find("main").Value;
			AcsTopRecord val = new AcsTopRecord(grdSensitivityResultsSummary.AdbTopProvider.GetActiveAdbTop());
			string[] array = obj.Split('.');
			AcsFlowsheet val2 = val.Flowsheet;
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 2; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				if (array[i] == "Blocks")
				{
					val2 = val2.Blocks.ValueAt(array[i + 1]).SubFlowsheets.ValueAt("Simulation");
					i += 2;
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			sensitivity = val2.Sensitivity.ValueAt(array[array.Length - 1]);
			if (flag)
			{
				grdSensitivityResultsSummary.IndexerPath = "@main1.SharedData.SubFlowsheets.Simulation.Sensitivity.@id.rows";
			}
			else
			{
				grdSensitivityResultsSummary.IndexerPath = "@main1.SharedData.Flowsheet.Sensitivity.@id.rows";
			}
			grdSensitivityResultsSummary.Fields.Clear();
			if (sensitivity != null && ((AcsArrayBase)sensitivity.rows).Length > 0)
			{
				int length = ((AcsArrayBase)sensitivity.columnHeaders).Length;
				_ = ((AcsArrayBase)sensitivity.rows).Length;
				AdsField item = new AdsField
				{
					IsFrozen = false,
					Width = 80.0,
					PropertyPath = "caseNo",
					Key = "caseNumberColumnKey",
					Label = ApwnResources.MMSensitivi_Result_Summar_MMFLabel0
				};
				grdSensitivityResultsSummary.Fields.Add(item);
				item = new AdsField
				{
					IsFrozen = false,
					Width = 80.0,
					PropertyPath = "rowStatus",
					Key = "rowStatusColumnKey",
					Label = ApwnResources.MMSensitivi_Result_Summar_MMFLabel1
				};
				item.convert = new ConvertDelegate(convertStatusColumn);
				grdSensitivityResultsSummary.Fields.Add(item);
				item = new AdsField
				{
					IsFrozen = false,
					Width = 80.0,
					PropertyPath = "description",
					Key = "descriptionColumnKey",
					Label = ApwnResources.MMSensitivi_Result_Summar_MMFLabel2
				};
				grdSensitivityResultsSummary.Fields.Add(item);
				for (int j = 1; j <= length; j++)
				{
					item = new AdsField
					{
						IsFrozen = false,
						Width = 80.0
					};
					item.Key = "columnKey" + j;
					item.PropertyPath = "values." + j;
					item.Label = sensitivity.columnHeaders.ValueAt(j).line1 + Environment.NewLine + sensitivity.columnHeaders.ValueAt(j).line2 + Environment.NewLine + sensitivity.columnHeaders.ValueAt(j).line3 + Environment.NewLine + sensitivity.columnHeaders.ValueAt(j).line4 + Environment.NewLine + sensitivity.columnHeaders.ValueAt(j).line5 + Environment.NewLine + sensitivity.columnHeaders.ValueAt(j).line6 + Environment.NewLine + sensitivity.columnHeaders.ValueAt(j).line7;
					grdSensitivityResultsSummary.Fields.Add(item);
				}
				grdSensitivityResultsSummary.UpdateDataGrid();
			}
		}

		private object convertStatusColumn(object input)
		{
			if (input.GetType() == typeof(int))
			{
				return (int)input switch
				{
					0 => "OK", 
					1 => "Errors", 
					2 => "Warnings", 
					_ => null, 
				};
			}
			return null;
		}

			}
		}

		public class MMCalcul_Input_Define_Converter : MMViewModel, IValueConverter
		{
			public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				string result = "";
				string text = "";
				if (value == null)
				{
					return result;
				}
				string text2 = value.ToString();
				string text3 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE." + text2, "value"));
				if (text3 == null)
				{
					return result;
				}
				foreach (VarType varType in VarTypes)
				{
					if (text3 == varType.UCase)
					{
						text = varType.MCase;
						break;
					}
				}
				foreach (PromptType prompt in Prompts)
				{
					string text4 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text2, "value"));
					string text5 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text2, "can_enter"));
					if (text5 == null)
					{
						text5 = "0";
					}
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4) && (!prompt.HideWhenLocked || text5 == "1"))
					{
						if (prompt.ConvertToMixedCase)
						{
							text4 = VarAccessing.StrConvMixCase(text4);
						}
						text = text + " " + prompt.Label + text4;
					}
				}
				return text;
			}

			public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
		}

		public static readonly CxvPropertyDeclaration grdVarList_GridData_Property = CxvPropertyDeclaration.Register("grdVarList_GridData", typeof(MMSensitivi_VarList_ViewModel), (CxvPropertyBase)new CxvVectorProperty
		{
			ItemType = typeof(grdVarList_GridData_ItemTemplate),
			IndexBasis = (CxvIndexBasisBase)new CxvChildCountIndexBasis
			{
				DataPath = "@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE",
				UseChildId = true,
				ProxyRow = false,
				Name = "y"
			}
		});

		public static List<VarType> VarTypes;

		public static List<PromptType> Prompts;

		public object grdVarList_GridData => ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(grdVarList_GridData_Property);

		protected override void OnUpdateData()
		{
			((MMTabPageViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
			if (((CxvViewModel)this).IsInitialUpdate)
			{
				Prompts = VarAccessing.VAPromptInit();
				VarTypes = VarAccessing.VAVarTypeInit(CxsConverter.ValueToStringCollection(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE", "option_list")));
			}
		}
	}

	public class MultiCaseExportConfiguration : Window, IComponentConnector
	{
		public enum ScenarioType
		{
			CaseStudy,
			ROM,
			Unified
		}

		internal System.Windows.Controls.RadioButton CaseStudyRB;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.RadioButton UnifiedRB;

		internal System.Windows.Controls.TextBox UnifiedTextbox;

		private bool _contentLoaded;

		public ScenarioType ExportType { get; private set; }

		public bool ExportMethod { get; private set; }

		public string UnifiedJsonFilePath { get; private set; }

		public MultiCaseExportConfiguration()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			CaseStudyRB.IsChecked = true;
		}

		private void SelectScenarioType(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			switch (((System.Windows.Controls.RadioButton)sender).Tag.ToString())
			{
			case "0":
				ExportMethod = false;
				ExportType = ScenarioType.CaseStudy;
				break;
			case "1":
				ExportMethod = false;
				ExportType = ScenarioType.ROM;
				break;
			case "2":
				ExportMethod = false;
				ExportType = ScenarioType.Unified;
				break;
			default:
				ExportMethod = true;
				ExportType = ScenarioType.CaseStudy;
				break;
			}
		}

		private void UnifiedOpenFile_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			System.Windows.Forms.OpenFileDialog openFileDialog = new System.Windows.Forms.OpenFileDialog();
			openFileDialog.Filter = "Json Files|*.json";
			if (openFileDialog.ShowDialog() == System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult.OK)
			{
				System.Windows.Controls.TextBox unifiedTextbox = UnifiedTextbox;
				string text = (UnifiedJsonFilePath = openFileDialog.FileName);
				unifiedTextbox.Text = text;
			}
		}

		private void Export_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.DialogResult = true;
			Close();
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		public void InitializeComponent()
		{
			if (!_contentLoaded)
			{
				_contentLoaded = true;
				Uri resourceLocator = new Uri("/AspenTech.AspenPlus.MMForms.ModelAnalysis;component/sensitivity/multicaseexportconfiguration.xaml", UriKind.Relative);
				System.Windows.Application.LoadComponent(this, resourceLocator);
			}
		}

		[DebuggerNonUserCode]
		[GeneratedCode("PresentationBuildTasks", "4.0.0.0")]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		void IComponentConnector.Connect(int connectionId, object target)
		{
			switch (connectionId)
			{
			case 1:
				CaseStudyRB = (System.Windows.Controls.RadioButton)target;
				CaseStudyRB.Checked += SelectScenarioType;
				break;
			case 2:
				((System.Windows.Controls.RadioButton)target).Checked += SelectScenarioType;
				break;
			case 3:
				UnifiedRB = (System.Windows.Controls.RadioButton)target;
				UnifiedRB.Checked += SelectScenarioType;
				break;
			case 4:
				UnifiedTextbox = (System.Windows.Controls.TextBox)target;
				break;
			case 5:
				((System.Windows.Controls.Button)target).Click += UnifiedOpenFile_Click;
				break;
			case 6:
				((System.Windows.Controls.RadioButton)target).Checked += SelectScenarioType;
				break;
			case 7:
				((System.Windows.Controls.Button)target).Click += Export_Click;
				break;
			default:
				_contentLoaded = true;
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	internal class VarAccessing
	{
		public static List<VarType> VAVarTypeInit(Collection<string> oplist)
		{
			List<VarType> list = new List<VarType>();
			list.Add(new VarType("Balance-Var", "BALANCE-VAR"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Bi-Cor-El", "BI-COR-EL"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Bi-Cor-Vec", "BI-COR-VEC"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Bi-Param", "BI-PARAM"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Block-Var", "BLOCK-VAR"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Block-Vec", "BLOCK-VEC"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Byprod-Var", "BYPROD-VAR"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Capital-Var", "CAPITAL-VAR"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Cblock-Var", "CBLOCK-VAR"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Chem-Var", "CHEM-VAR"));

			list.Add(new VarType("Stream-Var", "STREAM-VAR"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Stream-Vec", "STREAM-VEC"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Substrm-Vec", "SUBSTRM-VEC"));
			list.Add(new VarType("T-Vec", "TVEC"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Unary-Cor-El", "UNARY-COR-EL"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Unary-Param", "UNARY-PARAM"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Unit-Var", "UNIT-VAR"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Utility-Var", "UTILITY-VAR"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Un-Cor-Vec", "UN-COR-VEC"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Waste-Var", "WASTE-VAR"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Work-Cap-Var", "WORK-CAP-VAR"));
			list.Add(new VarType("Work-Power", "WORK-POWER"));
			if (oplist != null)
			{
				List<VarType> list2 = new List<VarType>();
				List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
				foreach (VarType item in list)
				{
					if (oplist.Contains(item.UCase))
					{
						list3.Add(item.UCase);
						list2.Add(item);
					}
				}
				{
					foreach (string item2 in oplist)
					{
						if (!list3.Contains(item2))
						{
							list2.Add(new VarType(StrConvMixCase(item2), item2));
						}
					}
					return list2;
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		public static string StrConvMixCase(string inputValue)
		{
			if (double.TryParse(inputValue, out var _))
			{
				return inputValue;
			}
			string[] array = inputValue.Split('-');
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			string[] array2 = array;
			foreach (string text in array2)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text.Trim()))
				{
					if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append("-");
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(text.Substring(0, 1).ToUpper() + text.Substring(1).ToLower());
				}
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static string VAVarGetDescription(MMViewModel viewModel, string basePath, string row, string typeName, List<PromptType> prompts, List<VarType> varTypes)
		{
			string text = null;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			string pathValue = GetPathValue(viewModel, basePath, typeName, row);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pathValue))
			{
				text = VAVarFindMCase(varTypes, pathValue);
				stringBuilder.Append(text);
			}
			foreach (PromptType prompt in prompts)
			{
				pathValue = GetPathValue(viewModel, basePath, prompt.Variable, row);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pathValue))
				{
					int valueOrDefault = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)viewModel).ExpandDataPathAndGet(basePath + "." + prompt.Variable + "." + row, "can_enter")).GetValueOrDefault();
					if (!prompt.HideWhenLocked || valueOrDefault == 1)
					{
						text = ((!prompt.ConvertToMixedCase) ? pathValue : StrConvMixCase(pathValue));
						stringBuilder.Append(" ").Append(prompt.Label).Append(text);
					}
				}
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private static string GetPathValue(MMViewModel viewModel, string basePath, string typeName, string row)
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)viewModel).ExpandDataPathAndGet(basePath + "." + typeName + "." + row, "value"));
		}

		private static string VAVarFindMCase(List<VarType> varTypes, string ucase)
		{
			List<string> list = (from v in varTypes
				where v.UCase == ucase
				select v.MCase).ToList();
			if (list != null && list.Count > 0)
			{
				return list[0];
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static List<VarType> VAGetVarIndex(List<VarType> VarTypes, ObjType oType)
		{
			List<VarType> list = new List<VarType>();
			switch (oType)
			{
			case ObjType.otAll:
				list = VarTypes;
				break;
			case ObjType.otBlock:
				foreach (VarType VarType in VarTypes)
				{
					if (VarType.UCase == "BLOCK-VAR" || VarType.UCase == "BLOCK-VEC" || VarType.UCase == "MEAS-VAR" || VarType.UCase == "CONT-VAR" || VarType.UCase == "CONFIG-VAR")
					{
						list.Add(VarType);
					}
				}
				break;
			case ObjType.otStream:
				foreach (VarType VarType2 in VarTypes)
				{
					if (VarType2.UCase == "STREAM-VAR" || VarType2.UCase == "MOLE-FLOW" || VarType2.UCase == "MOLE-FRAC" || VarType2.UCase == "MASS-FLOW" || VarType2.UCase == "MASS-FRAC" || VarType2.UCase == "STDVOL-FLOW" || VarType2.UCase == "STDVOL-FRAC" || VarType2.UCase == "STREAM-VEC" || VarType2.UCase == "STREAM-PROP" || VarType2.UCase == "SUBSTRM-VEC" || VarType2.UCase == "COMPATTR-VAR" || VarType2.UCase == "COMPATTR-VEC" || VarType2.UCase == "SUBSATTR-VAR" || VarType2.UCase == "SUBSATTR-VEC" || VarType2.UCase == "HEAT-DUTY" || VarType2.UCase == "WORK-POWER" || VarType2.UCase == "HEAT-TEMP" || VarType2.UCase == "HEAT-TEND" || VarType2.UCase == "QVEC" || VarType2.UCase == "TVEC" || VarType2.UCase == "STREAM-COST" || VarType2.UCase == "POWER-POWER")
					{
						list.Add(VarType2);
					}
				}
				break;
			case ObjType.otUtility:
				foreach (VarType VarType3 in VarTypes)
				{
					if (VarType3.UCase == "PARAMETER" || VarType3.UCase == "LOCAL-PARAM" || VarType3.UCase == "BALANCE-VAR" || VarType3.UCase == "PRESR-VAR" || VarType3.UCase == "UTILITY")
					{
						list.Add(VarType3);
					}
				}
				break;
			case ObjType.otProperty:
				foreach (VarType VarType4 in VarTypes)
				{
					if (VarType4.UCase == "UNARY-PARAM" || VarType4.UCase == "UNARY-COR-EL" || VarType4.UCase == "UN-COR-VEC" || VarType4.UCase == "BI-PARAM" || VarType4.UCase == "BI-COR-EL" || VarType4.UCase == "BI-COR-VEC" || VarType4.UCase == "NC-PARAM" || VarType4.UCase == "NC-COR-EL" || VarType4.UCase == "NC-COR-VEC" || VarType4.UCase == "GROUP-PARAM" || VarType4.UCase == "GROUP-BI-PAR" || VarType4.UCase == "GR-BI-COR-EL")
					{
						list.Add(VarType4);
					}
				}
				break;
			case ObjType.otReactions:
				foreach (VarType VarType5 in VarTypes)
				{
					if (VarType5.UCase == "CHEM-VAR" || VarType5.UCase == "REACT-VAR")
					{
						list.Add(VarType5);
					}
				}
				break;
			case ObjType.otCosting:
				foreach (VarType VarType6 in VarTypes)
				{
					if (VarType6.UCase == "BYPROD-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "CAPITAL-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "CBLOCK-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "LABOR-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "OPER-COST-VA" || VarType6.UCase == "PRODUCT-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "PROFIT-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "RAW-MAT-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "STARTUP-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "UNIT-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "UTILITY-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "WASTE-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "WORK-CAP-VAR")
					{
						list.Add(VarType6);
					}
				}
				break;
			case ObjType.otSensor:
				foreach (VarType VarType7 in VarTypes)
				{
					if (VarType7.UCase == "SENSOR-VAR")
					{
						list.Add(VarType7);
					}
				}
				break;
			case ObjType.otOther:
				foreach (VarType VarType8 in VarTypes)
				{
					if (VarType8.UCase == "MEAS-VAR" || VarType8.UCase == "CONT-VAR")
					{
						list.Add(VarType8);
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			return list;
		}

		public static int VAVarFindUCaseIndex(List<VarType> VarTypes, string UCase)
		{
			int result = -1;
			int num = 0;
			foreach (VarType VarType in VarTypes)
			{
				if (UCase == VarType.UCase)
				{
					result = num;
					break;
				}
				num++;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class VarType
	{
		public string MCase { get; set; }

		public string UCase { get; set; }

		public VarType(string mcase, string ucase)
		{
			MCase = mcase;
			UCase = ucase;
		}
	}
	public class PromptType
	{
		public string Text { get; set; }

		public string Label { get; set; }

		public string Variable { get; set; }

		public bool HideWhenLocked { get; set; }

		public bool ConvertToMixedCase { get; set; }
	}
	public enum ObjType
	{
		otAll,
		otBlock,
		otStream,
		otUtility,
		otProperty,
		otReactions,
		otCosting,
		otSensor,
		otOther
	}

		private void HideExtension()
		{
			for (int i = 0; i <= 7; i++)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("Rowsvw_" + i + "_Visible", (object)"Hidden");
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("Rowsvw_" + i + "_Find_Visible", (object)"Collapsed");
			}
		}

		internal void Display2(string varName = null)
		{
			if (_suppressDisplay2)
			{
				return;
			}
			_suppressDisplay2 = true;
			if (varName != null)
			{
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("xVarName", (object)varName);
			}
			HideExtension();
			byte b = 0;
			foreach (PromptType prompt in Prompts)
			{
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + ".@xVarName", "can_enter"));
				if (text == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + ".@xVarName", "value"));
				if (text2 != null && (text == "1" || (prompt.Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Units && text2 != "")))
				{
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("Rowsvw_" + b + "_Visible", (object)"Visible");
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("Rowsvw_" + b + "_Caption", (object)prompt.Text);
					object obj = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + ".@xVarName", "option_list");
					if (obj == null || obj.GetType() == typeof(DBNull))
					{
						((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("Rowsvw_" + b + "_Combo_Visible", (object)"Hidden");
						((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("Rowsvw_" + b + "_Text_Visible", (object)"Visible");
					}
					else
					{
						((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("Rowsvw_" + b + "_Combo_Visible", (object)"Visible");
						((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("Rowsvw_" + b + "_Text_Visible", (object)"Hidden");
					}
					string text3 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("Rowsvw_" + b + "_Combo_Visible"));
					if (prompt.Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Variable && text3 == "Visible")
					{
						((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("Rowsvw_" + b + "_Find_Visible", (object)"Visible");
					}
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("vwrow" + b, (object)prompt.Variable);
					b++;
					if (b > 7)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			_suppressDisplay2 = false;
		}

		protected override void OnSubPropertyDataChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration pd, string subPropertyName)
		{
			((CxvViewModel)this).OnSubPropertyDataChanged(pd, subPropertyName);
			if (!(subPropertyName == "Value"))
			{
				return;
			}
			string name = pd.Name;
			if (name == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			int length = name.Length;
			if (length != 16)
			{
				return;
			}
			switch (name[10])
			{
			case '0':
				if (!(name == "cboRowsvw_0_var1"))
				{
					break;
				}
				goto IL_00d6;
			case '1':
				if (!(name == "cboRowsvw_1_var1"))
				{
					break;
				}
				goto IL_00d6;
			case '2':
				if (!(name == "cboRowsvw_2_var1"))
				{
					break;
				}
				goto IL_00d6;
			case '3':
				if (!(name == "cboRowsvw_3_var1"))
				{
					break;
				}
				goto IL_00d6;
			case '4':
				if (!(name == "cboRowsvw_4_var1"))
				{
					break;
				}
				goto IL_00d6;
			case '5':
				if (!(name == "cboRowsvw_5_var1"))
				{
					break;
				}
				goto IL_00d6;
			case '6':
				if (!(name == "cboRowsvw_6_var1"))
				{
					break;
				}
				goto IL_00d6;
			case '7':
				{

		public class grdVary_GridData_ItemTemplate : MMViewModel
		{
			public static readonly CxvPropertyDeclaration active_var1_Property;

			public static readonly CxvPropertyDeclaration txtVarName_var1_Property;

			public static CxvPropertyDeclaration chkActive_var1_Property;

			private readonly CxvPropertyWatch watch = new CxvPropertyWatch();

			public static readonly CxvPropertyDeclaration txtLower_Property;

			public static readonly CxvPropertyDeclaration txtUpper_Property;

			public static readonly CxvPropertyDeclaration txtStep_Property;

			public static readonly CxvPropertyDeclaration txtMaxStep_Property;

			public static readonly CxvPropertyDeclaration cmbVaryUnit_Property;

			public static CxvPropertyDeclaration LabelIndex_Property;

			public object active_var1 => ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(active_var1_Property);

			public object txtVarName_var1 => ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(txtVarName_var1_Property);

			public object chkActive_var1 => ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(chkActive_var1_Property);

			public object txtVaryVariable => (object)new CxvManualVariableData
			{
				IsReadOnly = true,
				Value = VaryVar
			};

			public object txtCurrentValue => (object)new CxvManualVariableData
			{
				IsReadOnly = true,
				Value = CurrentValue
			};

			public string VaryVar
			{
				get
				{
					string result = "";
					string text = "";
					string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("y"));
					watch.PropertyDependencyChanged -= watch_PropertyDependencyChanged;
					watch.Clear();
					string text3 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE." + text2, "value", (string)null, watch));
					if (text3 == null)
					{
						return result;
					}
					foreach (VarType varType in VarTypes)
					{
						if (text3 == varType.UCase)
						{
							text = varType.MCase;
							break;
						}
					}
					foreach (PromptType prompt in Prompts)
					{
						if (prompt.Variable == "VARYPHYS_QTY" || prompt.Variable == "VARYUOM")
						{
							continue;
						}
						string text4 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text2, "value", (string)null, watch));
						string text5 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text2, "can_enter", (string)null, watch));
						if (text5 == null)
						{
							text5 = "0";
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4) && (!prompt.HideWhenLocked || text5 == "1"))
						{
							if (prompt.ConvertToMixedCase)
							{
								text4 = VarAccessing.StrConvMixCase(text4);
							}
							text = text + " " + prompt.Label + text4;
						}
					}
					result = text;
					if (!watch.IsEmpty)
					{
						watch.PropertyDependencyChanged += watch_PropertyDependencyChanged;
					}
					return result;
				}
			}

			public string CurrentValue
			{
				get
				{
					string result = "";
					string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("y"));
					string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE." + text, "value", (string)null, watch));
					if (text2 == null)
					{
						return result;
					}
					foreach (VarType varType in VarTypes)
					{
						if (text2 == varType.UCase)
						{
							_ = varType.MCase;
							break;
						}
					}
					string text3 = "Top.appModelV8.";
					foreach (PromptType prompt in Prompts)
					{
						if (prompt.Variable == "VARYBLOCK")
						{
							string text4 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text, "value", (string)null, watch));
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
							{
								if (text4.Contains("."))
								{
									string[] array = text4.Split('.');
									for (int i = 0; i < array.Count(); i++)
									{
										text3 = ((i != array.Count() - 1) ? (text3 + "Blocks." + array[i] + ".Data.") : (text3 + "Blocks." + array[i]));
									}
									text3 += ".Input.";
								}
								else
								{
									text3 = text3 + "Blocks." + text4 + ".Input.";
								}
							}
						}
						if (prompt.Variable == "VARYVARIABLE")
						{
							string text5 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text, "value", (string)null, watch));
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5))
							{
								text5 = text5.Replace("-", "_");
								text3 += text5;
							}
						}
						if (prompt.Variable == "VARYID1")
						{
							string text6 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text, "value", (string)null, watch));
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text6))
							{
								text3 = text3 + "." + text6;
							}
						}
						if (prompt.Variable == "VARYID2")
						{
							string text7 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text, "value", (string)null, watch));
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text7))
							{
								text3 = text3 + "." + text7;
							}
						}
						if (prompt.Variable == "VARYID3")
						{
							string text8 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text, "value", (string)null, watch));
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text8))
							{
								text3 = text3 + "." + text8;
							}
						}
					}
					return CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(text3, "value", (string)null, watch));
				}
			}

			public object txtLower => ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(txtLower_Property);

			public object txtUpper => ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(txtUpper_Property);

			public object txtStep => ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(txtStep_Property);

			public object cmbVaryUnit => ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(cmbVaryUnit_Property);

			public List<PromptType> Prompts
			{
				get
				{
					if (((CxvViewModel)this).Parent is MMOptimizat_Input_Vary_ViewModel mMOptimizat_Input_Vary_ViewModel)
					{
						return mMOptimizat_Input_Vary_ViewModel._prompts;
					}
					return null;
				}
			}

			public List<VarType> VarTypes
			{
				get
				{
					if (((CxvViewModel)this).Parent is MMOptimizat_Input_Vary_ViewModel mMOptimizat_Input_Vary_ViewModel)
					{
						return mMOptimizat_Input_Vary_ViewModel._varTypes;
					}
					return null;
				}
			}

			public object LabelIndex => ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(LabelIndex_Property);

			private void watch_PropertyDependencyChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
			{
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("txtVaryVariable");
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("txtCurrentValue");
			}

			public void RefreshVaryVar()
			{
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("txtVaryVariable");
			}

		public ObservableCollection<int> SelectedRowIndexes
		{
			get
			{
				return _selectedRowIndexes;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != _selectedRowIndexes)
				{
					if (_selectedRowIndexes != null)
					{
						_selectedRowIndexes.CollectionChanged -= _selectedRowIndexes_CollectionChanged;
					}
					_selectedRowIndexes = value;
					if (_selectedRowIndexes != null)
					{
						_selectedRowIndexes.CollectionChanged += _selectedRowIndexes_CollectionChanged;
					}
					RefreshButtonStatus();
					UpdateEditDetail();
					((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("SelectedRowIndexes");
				}
			}
		}

		private void _selectedRowIndexes_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			RefreshButtonStatus();
			UpdateEditDetail();
		}

		private void UpdateEditDetail()
		{
			if (((MMViewModel)this).View != null && SelectedRowIndexes != null && SelectedRowIndexes.Count == 1)
			{
				int num = SelectedRowIndexes[0];
				string text = $"#{num}";
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("varyno", (object)text);
			}
		}

		private int GetRowCount()
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveViewPropertyPath("grdVary_GridData.Count", _watcher)).GetValueOrDefault();
		}

		private bool CanMove(bool up)
		{
			return MultipleMovingHelper.CanMove(GetRowCount(), (IEnumerable<int>)SelectedRowIndexes, up, true);
		}

		private void MoveSelection(bool up)
		{
			if (CanMove(up))
			{
				int rowCount = GetRowCount();
				IDictionary<int, int> movingTargets = MultipleMovingHelper.GetMovingTargets(rowCount, (IEnumerable<int>)SelectedRowIndexes, up, true);
				IList<int> newIndexesAfterMoved = MultipleMovingHelper.GetNewIndexesAfterMoved(rowCount, movingTargets, true);
				((MMViewModel)this).ReorderChildren("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", (IEnumerable<int>)newIndexesAfterMoved);
				SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int>(movingTargets.Values);
				RefreshButtonStatus();
			}
		}

		private void RefreshButtonStatus()
		{
			cmdDelete_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdCopy_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdPaste_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdReorder_0_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdReorder_1_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
		}

		public override void Unload()
		{
			if (_watcher != null)
			{
				_watcher.PropertyDependencyChanged -= _watcher_PropertyDependencyChanged;
				_watcher = null;
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).Unload();
		}

		private void _watcher_PropertyDependencyChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			cmdDelete_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdCopy_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdPaste_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdReorder_0_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdReorder_1_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
		}

		private void cmdFind_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("varyno"));
			if (text.Length > 1 && int.TryParse(text.Substring(1), out var result))
			{
				result++;
				MmgExtensions.GxSearchVariable((MMViewModel)(object)this, ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main"), result, "", "", "VARY");
				Display2();
				((MMViewModel)this).UpdateDisplay((object)null);
			}
		}

		private void cmdNew_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			int valueOrDefault = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.SCRUD", "child_count")).GetValueOrDefault();
			((MMViewModel)this).AddChild("@main.Input.SCRUD", valueOrDefault);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet("@main.Input.SCRUD.#" + valueOrDefault, "value", (object)(valueOrDefault + 1));
			SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int> { valueOrDefault };
			UpdateEditDetail();
		}

		private void cmdDelete_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			IOrderedEnumerable<int> orderedEnumerable = _selectedRowIndexes.OrderBy((int index) => index);
			IEnumerable<string> enumerable = from index in _selectedRowIndexes
				let dataList = grdVary_GridData as IList
				let itemTemplate = dataList[index] as grdVary_GridData_ItemTemplate
				orderby index
				let strName = ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)itemTemplate, "txtVarName_var1.Value") as string
				select (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(strName)) ? strName : $"#{index + 1}";
			((MMViewModel)this).DeleteChildren("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", (IEnumerable<int>)orderedEnumerable, enumerable, false);
			RefreshButtonStatus();
		}

		private void MsgBox(out ShowMessageBoxEventArgs args, string variableName)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0027: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			args = new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
			{
				Message = Resources.MsgBox_Message2 + variableName + " ? ",
				Title = Resources.MsgBox_Title_Delete_Variable,
				MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.OKCancel
			};
		}

		private bool cmdDelete_CommandCanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIndexes != null && SelectedRowIndexes.Count > 0)
			{
				if (SelectedRowIndexes.Count == 1)
				{
					return SelectedRowIndexes[0] != GetRowCount();
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void cmdCopy_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			MmgExtensions.CopyObjectRows((MMViewModel)(object)this, text, "SENSITIVI.VARY", (IEnumerable<int>)SelectedRowIndexes);
			System.Windows.IDataObject dataObject = GridControlClipboardHelper.CloneClipboardDataObject();
			(((MMViewModel)this).View as MMOptimizat_Input_Vary).grdVary.DoDefaultCopy();
			System.Windows.IDataObject dataObject2 = GridControlClipboardHelper.CloneClipboardDataObject();
			GridControlClipboardHelper.CombineDataObjects(dataObject, dataObject2);
			cmdPaste_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
		}

		private bool cmdCopy_CommandCanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIndexes != null && SelectedRowIndexes.Count > 0)
			{
				if (SelectedRowIndexes.Count == 1)
				{
					return SelectedRowIndexes[0] != GetRowCount();
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void cmdPaste_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			ExecutePaste();
		}

		public bool ExecutePaste()
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (_isPastingObjectRows)
			{
				string[] oldIds = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", "child_ids"));
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
				result = ((SelectedRowIndexes == null || SelectedRowIndexes.Count != 1) ? (MmgExtensions.PasteObjectRows((MMViewModel)(object)this, text, "SENSITIVI.VARY") != 0) : (MmgExtensions.PasteObjectRows((MMViewModel)(object)this, text, "SENSITIVI.VARY", SelectedRowIndexes[0]) != 0));
				UpdateSelectedRow(oldIds);
			}
			else
			{
				string asiFormatTextFromClipboard = ClipboardHelper.GetAsiFormatTextFromClipboard();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(asiFormatTextFromClipboard))
				{
					try
					{
						IList<string> list = ClipboardHelper.ParseCxsPathFromAsiFormat(asiFormatTextFromClipboard, ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.Case.CaseFullFilePath, 1000000);
						result = ((MMViewModel)this).PasteVariable((IEnumerable<string>)list);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
			}
			RefreshButtonStatus();
			return result;
		}

		private void UpdateSelectedRow(IEnumerable<string> oldIds)
		{
			string[] array = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", "child_ids"));
			if (array == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = ((oldIds == null) ? array.FirstOrDefault() : array.Except(oldIds).FirstOrDefault());
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return;
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("varyno", (object)text);
			object obj = grdVary_GridData;
			CxvVectorPropertyData val = (CxvVectorPropertyData)((obj is CxvVectorPropertyData) ? obj : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < val.Count; i++)
			{
				if (val[i] is grdVary_GridData_ItemTemplate grdVary_GridData_ItemTemplate && string.Equals(((CxvViewModel)grdVary_GridData_ItemTemplate).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE.@y", "id") as string, text))
				{

		private void Display2()
		{
			int num = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < cmdFind_Visibilities.Length; i++)
			{
				cmdFind_Visibilities[i] = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_BlockVarVisible", (object)"0", true);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_HybridVarVisible", (object)"0", true);
			for (int j = 0; j < Prompts.Length; j++)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				int? num2 = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + Prompts[j].Variable + ".@varyno", "can_enter"));
				string value = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + Prompts[j].Variable + "..@varyno", "value"));
				flag = !num2.HasValue || num2.Value == 0;
				if (flag && (!(Prompts[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Units) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(value)))
				{
					continue;
				}
				_label_Visibilities[num] = Visibility.Visible;
				_label_captions[num] = Prompts[j].Text;
				string[] array = CxsConverter.ValueToOptionArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + Prompts[j].Variable + ".@varyno", "option_list"));
				if (array == null || array.Length == 0 || Prompts[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Sentence)
				{
					_combo_Visibilities[num] = Visibility.Collapsed;
					_textEdit_Visibilities[num] = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				else
				{
					_combo_Visibilities[num] = Visibility.Visible;
					_textEdit_Visibilities[num] = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				if (((MMViewModel)this).View is MMOptimizat_Input_Vary mMOptimizat_Input_Vary)
				{
					object obj = ((FrameworkElement)(object)mMOptimizat_Input_Vary).FindName("txtRows_" + num);
					MMValueEdit val = (MMValueEdit)((obj is MMValueEdit) ? obj : null);
					object obj2 = ((FrameworkElement)(object)mMOptimizat_Input_Vary).FindName("cboRows_" + num);
					MMComboEdit val2 = (MMComboEdit)((obj2 is MMComboEdit) ? obj2 : null);
					if (flag || Prompts[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Sentence)
					{
						if (val != null)
						{
							((AesControlBase)val).IsMMEnabled = false;
						}
						if (val2 != null)
						{
							((AesControlBase)val2).IsMMEnabled = false;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						if (val != null)
						{
							((AesControlBase)val).IsMMEnabled = true;
						}
						if (val2 != null)
						{
							((AesControlBase)val2).IsMMEnabled = true;
						}
					}
					if (Prompts[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Units && val2 != null && !((AesControlBase)val2).IsMMEnabled)
					{
						_combo_Visibilities[num] = Visibility.Collapsed;
						_textEdit_Visibilities[num] = Visibility.Visible;
					}
				}
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveViewPropertyPath("cboVarType_var1.Value", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
				if (Prompts[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Variable && _combo_Visibilities[num] == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					cmdFind_Visibilities[num] = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				else if (Prompts[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Variable && (text == "Block-Var" || text == "Sensor-Var"))
				{
					_combo_Visibilities[num] = Visibility.Collapsed;
					_textEdit_Visibilities[num] = Visibility.Collapsed;
					string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARYBLOCK.@varyno", "value"));
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
					{
						text2 = ((MMViewModel)this).GetFullBlockName("@main", text2);
						int modelCategoryByBlockName = ((MMViewModel)this).GetModelCategoryByBlockName(text2);
						if (text == "Sensor-Var" || modelCategoryByBlockName == 14)
						{
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_HybridVarVisible", (object)"1", true);
						}
						else
						{
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_BlockVarVisible", (object)"1", true);
						}
					}
				}
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter($"row{num}", (object)Prompts[j].Variable);
				num++;
				if (num > 7)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE.@varyno", "value"))))
			{
				num = 0;
			}
			for (int k = num; k <= 7; k++)
			{
				_label_Visibilities[k] = Visibility.Collapsed;
				_combo_Visibilities[k] = Visibility.Collapsed;
				_textEdit_Visibilities[k] = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("Label_Captions");
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("Label_Visibilities");
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("Combo_Visibilities");
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("TextEdit_Visibilities");
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("cmdFind_Visibilities");
		}

		protected override void OnUpdateData()
		{
			if (((CxvViewModel)this).IsInitialUpdate)
			{
				_watcher.PropertyDependencyChanged += _watcher_PropertyDependencyChanged;
				Collection<string> oplist = CxsConverter.ValueToStringCollection(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", "option_list"));
				_varTypes = VarAccessing.VAVarTypeInit(oplist);
				_prompts = VarAccessing.VAVaryPromptInit();
				Display2();
			}
			((MMTabPageViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
		}

		protected override void OnSubPropertyDataChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration pd, string subPropertyName)
		{
			if (subPropertyName == "Value")
			{
				Display2();
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).OnSubPropertyDataChanged(pd, subPropertyName);
		}

		protected override void OnViewChanged()
		{
			Display2();
			((MMViewModel)this).OnViewChanged();
		}

		public override bool PasteVariable(IEnumerable<string> paths)
		{
			if (paths != null && paths.Count() > 0)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				string[] oldIds = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", "child_ids"));
				foreach (string path in paths)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
					{
						SmgeExtensions.SMVariableAccessingParse((MMViewModel)(object)this, path, (VarAcessType)1, ref flag);
					}
				}
				UpdateSelectedRow(oldIds);
				return true;
			}
			return ((MMViewModel)this).PasteVariable(paths);
		}
	}

				{
					_isBlockActive = false;
				}

					statusWatch.PropertyDependencyChanged += statusWatch_PropertyDependencyChanged;
				}

				_isBlockActive = value;
