			public string VaryVar
			{
				get
				{
					string result = "";
					string text = "";
					string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("y"));
					watch.PropertyDependencyChanged -= watch_PropertyDependencyChanged;
					watch.Clear();
					string text3 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE." + text2, "value", (string)null, watch));
					if (text3 == null)
					{
						return result;
					}
					foreach (VarType varType in VarTypes)
					{
						if (text3 == varType.UCase)
						{
							text = varType.MCase;
							break;
						}
					}
					foreach (PromptType prompt in Prompts)
					{
						if (prompt.Variable == "VARYPHYS_QTY" || prompt.Variable == "VARYUOM")
						{
							continue;
						}
						string text4 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text2, "value", (string)null, watch));
						string text5 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text2, "can_enter", (string)null, watch));
						if (text5 == null)
						{
							text5 = "0";
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4) && (!prompt.HideWhenLocked || text5 == "1"))
						{
							if (prompt.ConvertToMixedCase)
							{
								text4 = VarAccessing.StrConvMixCase(text4);
							}
							text = text + " " + prompt.Label + text4;
						}
					}
					result = text;
					if (!watch.IsEmpty)
					{
						watch.PropertyDependencyChanged += watch_PropertyDependencyChanged;
					}
					return result;
				}
			}

			public string CurrentValue
			{
				get
				{
					string result = "";
					string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("y"));
					string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE." + text, "value", (string)null, watch));
					if (text2 == null)
					{
						return result;
					}
					foreach (VarType varType in VarTypes)
					{
						if (text2 == varType.UCase)
						{
							_ = varType.MCase;
							break;
						}
					}
					string text3 = "Top.appModelV8.";
					foreach (PromptType prompt in Prompts)
					{
						if (prompt.Variable == "VARYBLOCK")
						{
							string text4 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text, "value", (string)null, watch));
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4))
							{
								if (text4.Contains("."))
								{
									string[] array = text4.Split('.');
									for (int i = 0; i < array.Count(); i++)
									{
										text3 = ((i != array.Count() - 1) ? (text3 + "Blocks." + array[i] + ".Data.") : (text3 + "Blocks." + array[i]));
									}
									text3 += ".Input.";
								}
								else
								{
									text3 = text3 + "Blocks." + text4 + ".Input.";
								}
							}
						}
						if (prompt.Variable == "VARYVARIABLE")
						{
							string text5 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text, "value", (string)null, watch));
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text5))
							{
								text5 = text5.Replace("-", "_");
								text3 += text5;
							}
						}
						if (prompt.Variable == "VARYID1")
						{
							string text6 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text, "value", (string)null, watch));
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text6))
							{
								text3 = text3 + "." + text6;
							}
						}
						if (prompt.Variable == "VARYID2")
						{
							string text7 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text, "value", (string)null, watch));
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text7))
							{
								text3 = text3 + "." + text7;
							}
						}
						if (prompt.Variable == "VARYID3")
						{
							string text8 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text, "value", (string)null, watch));
							if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text8))
							{
								text3 = text3 + "." + text8;
							}
						}
					}
					return CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(text3, "value", (string)null, watch));
				}
			}

						_selectedRowIndexes.CollectionChanged -= _selectedRowIndexes_CollectionChanged;
					}
					_selectedRowIndexes = value;
					if (_selectedRowIndexes != null)
					{
						_selectedRowIndexes.CollectionChanged += _selectedRowIndexes_CollectionChanged;
					}
					((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("SelectedRowIndexes");
				}
			}
		}

		private void _selectedRowIndexes_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			RefreshButtonStatus();
			UpdateEditDetail();
		}

		private void UpdateEditDetail()
		{
			if (((MMViewModel)this).View != null && SelectedRowIndexes != null && SelectedRowIndexes.Count == 1)
			{
				int num = SelectedRowIndexes[0];
				string text = $"#{num}";
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("varyno", (object)text);
			}
		}

		private int GetRowCount()
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveViewPropertyPath("grdVary_GridData.Count", _watcher)).GetValueOrDefault();
		}

		private void RefreshButtonStatus()
		{
			cmdDelete_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdCopy_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdPaste_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
		}

		public override void Unload()
		{
			if (_watcher != null)
			{
				_watcher.PropertyDependencyChanged -= _watcher_PropertyDependencyChanged;
				_watcher = null;
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).Unload();
		}

		private void _watcher_PropertyDependencyChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			cmdDelete_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdCopy_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdPaste_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
		}

		private void cmdFind_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			Collection<string> collection = CxsConverter.ValueToStringCollection(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.SCRUD", "child_ids"));
			string item = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("varyno"));
			MmgExtensions.GxSearchVariable((MMViewModel)(object)this, ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main"), collection.IndexOf(item) + 1, "", "", "VARY");
			((MMViewModel)this).UpdateAll();
			((MMViewModel)this).UpdateDisplay((object)null);
		}

		private void cmdNew_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			int valueOrDefault = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.SCRUD", "child_count")).GetValueOrDefault();
			int num = valueOrDefault + 1;
			((MMViewModel)this).AddChild("@main.Input.SCRUD", num);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet("@main.Input.SCRUD.#" + valueOrDefault, "value", (object)num);
			SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int> { valueOrDefault };
			UpdateEditDetail();
		}

		private void cmdDelete_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			IOrderedEnumerable<int> orderedEnumerable = _selectedRowIndexes.OrderBy((int index) => index);
			IEnumerable<string> enumerable = from index in _selectedRowIndexes
				let dataList = grdVary_GridData as IList
				let itemTemplate = dataList[index] as grdVary_GridData_ItemTemplate
				orderby index
				let strName = ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)itemTemplate, "txtVarName_var1.Value") as string
				select (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(strName)) ? strName : $"#{index + 1}";
			((MMViewModel)this).DeleteChildren("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", (IEnumerable<int>)orderedEnumerable, enumerable, false);
			RefreshButtonStatus();
		}

		private void MsgBox(out ShowMessageBoxEventArgs args, string variableName)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0027: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			args = new ShowMessageBoxEventArgs
			{
				Message = Resources.MsgBox_Message2 + variableName + " ? ",
				Title = Resources.MsgBox_Title_Delete_Variable,
				MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.OKCancel
			};
		}

		private bool cmdDelete_CommandCanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIndexes != null && SelectedRowIndexes.Count > 0)
			{
				if (SelectedRowIndexes.Count == 1)
				{
					return SelectedRowIndexes[0] != GetRowCount();
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void cmdCopy_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
			MmgExtensions.CopyObjectRows((MMViewModel)(object)this, text, "SENSITIVI.VARY", (IEnumerable<int>)SelectedRowIndexes);
			cmdPaste_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
		}

		private bool cmdCopy_CommandCanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIndexes != null && SelectedRowIndexes.Count > 0)
			{
				if (SelectedRowIndexes.Count == 1)
				{
					return SelectedRowIndexes[0] != GetRowCount();
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void cmdPaste_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			ExecutePaste();
		}

		public bool ExecutePaste()
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (_isPastingObjectRows)
			{
				string[] oldIds = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", "child_ids"));
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
				result = ((SelectedRowIndexes == null || SelectedRowIndexes.Count != 1) ? (MmgExtensions.PasteObjectRows((MMViewModel)(object)this, text, "SENSITIVI.VARY") != 0) : (MmgExtensions.PasteObjectRows((MMViewModel)(object)this, text, "SENSITIVI.VARY", SelectedRowIndexes[0]) != 0));
				UpdateSelectedRow(oldIds);
			}
			else
			{
				string asiFormatTextFromClipboard = ClipboardHelper.GetAsiFormatTextFromClipboard();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(asiFormatTextFromClipboard))
				{
					try
					{
						IList<string> list = ClipboardHelper.ParseCxsPathFromAsiFormat(asiFormatTextFromClipboard, ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.Case.CaseFullFilePath, 1000000);
						result = ((MMViewModel)this).PasteVariable((IEnumerable<string>)list);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
			}
			RefreshButtonStatus();
			return result;
		}

		private void UpdateSelectedRow(IEnumerable<string> oldIds)
		{
			string[] array = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", "child_ids"));
			if (array == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = ((oldIds == null) ? array.FirstOrDefault() : array.Except(oldIds).FirstOrDefault());
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return;
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("varyno", (object)text);
			object obj = grdVary_GridData;
			CxvVectorPropertyData val = (CxvVectorPropertyData)((obj is CxvVectorPropertyData) ? obj : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < val.Count; i++)
			{
				if (val[i] is grdVary_GridData_ItemTemplate grdVary_GridData_ItemTemplate && string.Equals(((CxvViewModel)grdVary_GridData_ItemTemplate).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE.@y", "id") as string, text))
				{
					SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int>(new int[1] { i });
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private bool cmdPaste_CommandCanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (MmgExtensions.CanPasteObjectRows((MMViewModel)(object)this, "VARY"))
			{
				_isPastingObjectRows = true;
				return true;
			}
			if (((MMViewModel)this).CanPasteVariable())
			{
				_isPastingObjectRows = false;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		protected override void OnViewChanged()
		{
			Display2();
			((MMViewModel)this).OnViewChanged();
		}

		protected override void OnUpdateData()
		{
			if (((CxvViewModel)this).IsInitialUpdate)
			{
				_watcher.PropertyDependencyChanged += _watcher_PropertyDependencyChanged;
				Collection<string> oplist = CxsConverter.ValueToStringCollection(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", "option_list"));
				_varTypes = VarAccessing.VAVarTypeInit(oplist);
				_prompts = VarAccessing.VAVaryPromptInit();
				Display2();
			}
			((MMTabPageViewModel)this).OnUpdateData();
		}

		protected override void OnSubPropertyDataChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration pd, string subPropertyName)
		{
			((CxvViewModel)this).OnSubPropertyDataChanged(pd, subPropertyName);
			if (pd.Name == "mbvnum_var2" && subPropertyName == "Value")
			{
				if ((string)((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("varyno") == "<New>")
				{
					int? num = CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.SCRUD", "child_count"));
					((MMViewModel)this).AddChild("@main.Input.SCRUD", -1);
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("varyno", (object)("#" + num.Value));
				}
				Display2();
			}
			else if (subPropertyName == "Value")
			{
				Display2();
			}
		}

		private void Display2()
		{
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("varyno"));
			object obj = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE." + text, "value");
			int num = 0;
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_BlockVarVisible", (object)"0", true);
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_HybridVarVisible", (object)"0", true);
			for (int i = 0; i < cmdFind_Visibility.Count; i++)
			{
				cmdFind_Visibility[i] = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < Prompts.Count; j++)
				{
					string text2 = "@main.Input." + Prompts[j].Variable + "." + text;
					if (((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider == null || ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.Case == null || ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.Case.ResolveNode(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath(text2)) == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					bool? flag = CxsConverter.ValueToBoolean(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(text2, "can_enter"));
					if ((!flag.HasValue || !flag.Value) && (string.Compare(Prompts[j].Text, Resources.PromptType_Text_Units, StringComparison.Ordinal) != 0 || string.IsNullOrEmpty(CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(text2, "value")))))
					{
						continue;
					}
					lblrows_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Visible;
					lblrows_Caption[num] = Prompts[j].Text;
					object obj2 = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet(text2, "option_list");
					if (obj2 == null || obj2.GetType() == typeof(DBNull) || Prompts[j].Text == "Sentence:")
					{
						cboRows_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Collapsed;
						txtRows_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else
					{
						cboRows_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Visible;
						txtRows_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Collapsed;
					}
					if (Prompts[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Variable && cboRows_Visibility[num] == Visibility.Visible)
					{
						cmdFind_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Visible;
					}
					else if ((obj as string == "SENSOR-VAR" || obj as string == "BLOCK-VAR") && Prompts[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Variable)
					{
						cboRows_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Collapsed;
						txtRows_Visibility[num] = Visibility.Collapsed;
						string text3 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARYBLOCK.@varyno", "value"));
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
						{
							text3 = ((MMViewModel)this).GetFullBlockName("@main", text3);
							int modelCategoryByBlockName = ((MMViewModel)this).GetModelCategoryByBlockName(text3);
							if (obj as string == "SENSOR-VAR" || modelCategoryByBlockName == 14)
							{
								((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_HybridVarVisible", (object)"1", true);
							}
							else
							{
								((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_BlockVarVisible", (object)"1", true);
							}
						}
					}
					if (((MMViewModel)this).View is MMDataFit_Input_Vary mMDataFit_Input_Vary)
					{
						object obj3 = ((FrameworkElement)(object)mMDataFit_Input_Vary).FindName("txtRows_" + num);
						MMTextEdit val = (MMTextEdit)((obj3 is MMTextEdit) ? obj3 : null);
						object obj4 = ((FrameworkElement)(object)mMDataFit_Input_Vary).FindName("cboRows_" + num);
						MMComboEdit val2 = (MMComboEdit)((obj4 is MMComboEdit) ? obj4 : null);

		public override bool PasteVariable(IEnumerable<string> paths)
		{
			if (paths != null && paths.Count() > 0)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				string[] oldIds = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.VARY_VARTYPE", "child_ids"));

				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))

						SmgeExtensions.SMVariableAccessingParse((MMViewModel)(object)this, path, (VarAcessType)1, ref flag);
					}
				}
				UpdateSelectedRow(oldIds);
				return true;
			}
			return ((MMViewModel)this).PasteVariable(paths);
		}

			{
				ExposeAs = (CxvVariableTypeCode)6,
				DataPath = "@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE.@y",
				DisplayedCase = (CxsDisplayedCase)1,
				Sorted = true
			});

			private readonly CxvPropertyWatch watch = new CxvPropertyWatch();

			public static CxvPropertyDeclaration LabelIndex_Property = CxvPropertyDeclaration.Register("LabelIndex", typeof(grdDefine_GridData_ItemTemplate), (CxvPropertyBase)new CxvParameterProperty
			{
				Parameter = "y"
			});

			public object txtVarName_var1 => ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(txtVarName_var1_Property);

			public object txtVarDefn_VarDefn => (object)new CxvManualVariableData
			{
				IsReadOnly = true,
				Value = VarDefn
			};

			public string VarDefn
			{
				get
				{
					string result = "";
					string text = "";
					string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("y"));
					watch.PropertyDependencyChanged -= watch_PropertyDependencyChanged;
					watch.Clear();
					string text3 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE." + text2, "value", (string)null, watch));
					if (text3 == null)
					{
						return result;
					}
					foreach (VarType varType in VarTypes)
					{
						if (text3 == varType.UCase)
						{
							text = varType.MCase;
							break;
						}
					}
					foreach (PromptType prompt in Prompts)
					{
						string text4 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text2, "value", (string)null, watch));
						string text5 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + prompt.Variable + "." + text2, "can_enter", (string)null, watch));
						if (text5 == null)
						{
							text5 = "0";
						}
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text4) && (!prompt.HideWhenLocked || text5 == "1"))
						{
							if (prompt.ConvertToMixedCase)
							{
								text4 = VarAccessing.StrConvMixCase(text4);
							}
							text = text + " " + prompt.Label + text4;
						}
					}
					result = text;
					if (!watch.IsEmpty)
					{
						watch.PropertyDependencyChanged += watch_PropertyDependencyChanged;
					}
					return result;
				}
			}

			public List<PromptType> Prompts
			{
				get
				{
					if (((CxvViewModel)this).Parent is MMDataSet_Input_Define_ViewModel mMDataSet_Input_Define_ViewModel)
					{
						return mMDataSet_Input_Define_ViewModel._prompts;
					}
					return null;
				}
			}

			public List<VarType> VarTypes
			{
				get
				{
					if (((CxvViewModel)this).Parent is MMDataSet_Input_Define_ViewModel mMDataSet_Input_Define_ViewModel)
					{
						return mMDataSet_Input_Define_ViewModel._varTypes;
					}
					return null;
				}
			}

			public string Row => CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("y"));

			public object LabelIndex => ((CxvViewModel)this).GetValue(LabelIndex_Property);

			private void watch_PropertyDependencyChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
			{
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("txtVarDefn_VarDefn");
			}

			public void RefreshVarDefn()
			{
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("txtVarDefn_VarDefn");
			}
		}

		public class VRowMaskConverter : IValueConverter
		{
			public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				if (value as Type == typeof(double))
				{
					return (object)(AesMaskOption)2;
				}
				return (object)(AesMaskOption)0;
			}

			public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
			{
				throw new NotImplementedException();
			}
		}

		{

			}

		public string cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue
		{
			get
			{
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE.@xInt", "value"));
				_cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue = text;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue))
				{
					foreach (VarType item in VarAccessing.VAVarTypeInit(CxsConverter.ValueToStringCollection(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE", "option_list"))))
					{
						if (item.UCase == _cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue)
						{
							_cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue = item.MCase;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				return _cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue;
			}
			set
			{
				string text = _cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue;
				_cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue = value;
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue");
				if (!(_cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue != text))
				{
					return;
				}
				foreach (VarType item in VarAccessing.VAVarTypeInit(CxsConverter.ValueToStringCollection(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE", "option_list"))))
				{
					if (item.MCase == _cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue)
					{
						_cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue = item.UCase;
						break;
					}
				}
				((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE.@xInt", "value", (object)_cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue);
				Display2();
			}
		}

		private void _watcher_PropertyDependencyChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			cmdDelete_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdCopy_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdPaste_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdReorder_0_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdReorder_1_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
		}

			//IL_0042: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

			Collection<string> existingIds = CxsConverter.ValueToStringCollection(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARIABLE", "child_ids"));
			maskType val = (maskType)Enum.ToObject(typeof(maskType), (object)(maskType)3);

		private void cmdDelete_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (_selectedRowIndexes != null)
			{
				IEnumerable<int> source = _selectedRowIndexes.Where((int p) => p != GetRowCount() - 1);
				IOrderedEnumerable<int> orderedEnumerable = source.OrderBy((int index) => index);
				IEnumerable<string> enumerable = from index in source
					let dataList = grdDefine_GridData as IList
					let itemTemplate = dataList[index] as grdDefine_GridData_ItemTemplate
					orderby index
					let strName = ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)itemTemplate, "txtVarName_var1.Value") as string
					select (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(strName)) ? strName : $"#{index + 1}";
				((MMViewModel)this).DeleteChildren("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE", (IEnumerable<int>)orderedEnumerable, enumerable, false);
				RefreshButtonStatus();
			}
		}

				Title = Resources.MsgBox_Title_Delete_Variable,
				MsgBoxButton = MessageBoxButton.OKCancel
			};
		}

		private bool cmdDelete_CommandCanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (SelectedRowIndexes != null && SelectedRowIndexes.Count > 0)
			{
				if (SelectedRowIndexes.Count == 1)
				{
					return SelectedRowIndexes[0] != GetRowCount() - 1;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

			MmgExtensions.CopyObjectRows((MMViewModel)(object)this, text, "SENSITIVI.VARTYPE", (IEnumerable<int>)SelectedRowIndexes);
			cmdPaste_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
		}

			{
				if (SelectedRowIndexes.Count == 1)
				{
					return SelectedRowIndexes[0] != GetRowCount() - 1;

			}
			return false;
		}

		private void cmdPaste_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			ExecutePaste();
		}

		public bool ExecutePaste()
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (_isPastingObjectRows)
			{
				string[] oldIds = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE", "child_ids"));
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("main", (CxvPropertyWatch)null));
				result = ((((SelectedRowIndexes != null) ? SelectedRowIndexes.Count : 0) != 1) ? (MmgExtensions.PasteObjectRows((MMViewModel)(object)this, text, "SENSITIVI.VARTYPE") != 0) : (MmgExtensions.PasteObjectRows((MMViewModel)(object)this, text, "SENSITIVI.VARTYPE", SelectedRowIndexes[0]) != 0));
				UpdateSelectedRow(oldIds);
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue");
			}
			else
			{
				string asiFormatTextFromClipboard = ClipboardHelper.GetAsiFormatTextFromClipboard();
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(asiFormatTextFromClipboard))
				{
					try
					{
						IList<string> list = ClipboardHelper.ParseCxsPathFromAsiFormat(asiFormatTextFromClipboard, ((CxvViewModel)this).ResolvedCxsProvider.Case.CaseFullFilePath, 1000000);
						result = ((MMViewModel)this).PasteVariable((IEnumerable<string>)list);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
			}
			RefreshButtonStatus();
			return result;
		}

		private void UpdateSelectedRow(IEnumerable<string> oldIds)
		{
			string[] array = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE", "child_ids"));
			if (array == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = ((oldIds == null) ? array.FirstOrDefault() : array.Except(oldIds).FirstOrDefault());
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return;
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("xVarName", (object)text);
			object obj = grdDefine_GridData;
			CxvVectorPropertyData val = (CxvVectorPropertyData)((obj is CxvVectorPropertyData) ? obj : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < val.Count; i++)
			{
				if (val[i] is grdDefine_GridData_ItemTemplate grdDefine_GridData_ItemTemplate && string.Equals(((CxvViewModel)grdDefine_GridData_ItemTemplate).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE.@y", "id") as string, text))
				{
					SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int>(new int[1] { i });
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		private bool cmdPaste_CommandCanExecute(object parameter)
		{
			if (MmgExtensions.CanPasteObjectRows((MMViewModel)(object)this, "FVN"))
			{
				_isPastingObjectRows = true;
				return true;
			}
			if (((MMViewModel)this).CanPasteVariable())
			{
				_isPastingObjectRows = false;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void _selectedRowIndexes_CollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			RefreshButtonStatus();
			UpdateEditDetail();
		}

		private void UpdateEditDetail()
		{
			if (((MMViewModel)this).View != null && SelectedRowIndexes != null && SelectedRowIndexes.Count == 1)
			{
				int index = SelectedRowIndexes[0];
				string text = ICxvViewDataContainerExtensions.ResolveViewPropertyPath((ICxvViewDataContainer)(object)((grdDefine_GridData as IList)[index] as grdDefine_GridData_ItemTemplate), "txtVarName_var1.Value") as string;
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveParameter("xVarName"));
				}
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("xVarName", (object)text);
			}
		}

		private int GetRowCount()
		{
			return CxsConverter.ValueToInt32(((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveViewPropertyPath("grdDefine_GridData.Count", _watcher)).GetValueOrDefault();
		}

		private bool CanMove(bool up)
		{
			return MultipleMovingHelper.CanMove(GetRowCount(), (IEnumerable<int>)SelectedRowIndexes, up, false);
		}

		private void MoveSelection(bool up)
		{
			if (CanMove(up))
			{
				int rowCount = GetRowCount();
				IDictionary<int, int> movingTargets = MultipleMovingHelper.GetMovingTargets(rowCount, (IEnumerable<int>)SelectedRowIndexes, up, false);
				IList<int> newIndexesAfterMoved = MultipleMovingHelper.GetNewIndexesAfterMoved(rowCount, movingTargets, false);
				((MMViewModel)this).ReorderChildren("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE", (IEnumerable<int>)newIndexesAfterMoved);
				SelectedRowIndexes = new ObservableCollection<int>(movingTargets.Values);
				RefreshButtonStatus();
			}
		}

		private void RefreshButtonStatus()
		{
			cmdDelete_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdCopy_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdPaste_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdReorder_0_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
			cmdReorder_1_Command.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
		}

		private void cmdFind_CommandExecute(object parameter)
		{
			object obj = mnuVarname_ListVar;
			CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData val = (CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData)((obj is CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData) ? obj : null);
			string selectedItem = val.SelectedItem;
			int num = -1;
			for (int i = 0; i < val.Items.Count; i++)
			{
				if (val.Items[i].Display == selectedItem)
				{
					num = i;
					break;
				}
			}
			MmgExtensions.GxSearchVariable((MMViewModel)(object)this, ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPath("@main"), num + 1, selectedItem, "", "DEFINE");
			Display2();
			((MMViewModel)this).UpdateDisplay((object)null);
		}

		private void DoVarnameChange()
		{
			object obj = mnuVarname_ListVar;
			CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData val = (CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData)((obj is CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData) ? obj : null);
			if (val != null)
			{
				string selectedItem = val.SelectedItem;
				string optVal = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.OPT_CATEG." + selectedItem, "value"));
				UpdateVarTypeOptions(optVal);
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("xInt", (object)selectedItem);
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue");
			}
		}

		protected override void OnSubPropertyDataChanged(CxvPropertyDeclaration pd, string subPropertyName)
		{
			((CxvViewModel)this).OnSubPropertyDataChanged(pd, subPropertyName);
			if (subPropertyName == "Value" && pd.Name == "optCategory_MultiMap")
			{
				object obj = optCategory_MultiMap;
				CxvMultiMapPropertyData val = (CxvMultiMapPropertyData)((obj is CxvMultiMapPropertyData) ? obj : null);
				if (val != null && val.Value != null)
				{
					string optVal = val.Value.ToString();
					UpdateVarTypeOptions(optVal);
					Display2();
				}
			}
			else if (pd.Name == "mnuVarname_ListVar" && subPropertyName == "SelectedItem")
			{
				DoVarnameChange();
			}
			else if (subPropertyName == "Value")
			{
				Display2();
			}
		}

		private void UpdateVarTypeOptions(string optVal)
		{
			ObjType objType = optVal switch
			{
				"ALL" => ObjType.otAll, 
				"BLOCK" => ObjType.otBlock, 
				"STREAM" => ObjType.otStream, 
				"MODUT" => ObjType.otUtility, 
				"PROP" => ObjType.otProperty, 
				"REACT" => ObjType.otReactions, 
				"COST" => ObjType.otCosting, 
				_ => ObjType.otAll, 
			};
			List<VarType> list = VarAccessing.VAVarTypeInit(CxsConverter.ValueToStringCollection(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE", "option_list")));
			if (list == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (objType == ObjType.otAll)
			{
				if (cboVarTypevw_var2 == null)
				{
					cboVarTypevw_var2 = new ObservableCollection<string>();
				}
				cboVarTypevw_var2.Clear();
				{
					foreach (VarType item in list)
					{
						cboVarTypevw_var2.Add(item.MCase);
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			List<VarType> list2 = VAGetVarIndex(list, objType);
			if (cboVarTypevw_var2 == null)
			{
				cboVarTypevw_var2 = new ObservableCollection<string>();
			}
			cboVarTypevw_var2.Clear();
			foreach (VarType item2 in list2)
			{
				cboVarTypevw_var2.Add(item2.MCase);
			}
			object obj = mnuVarname_ListVar;
			CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData val = (CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData)((obj is CxvAdvCollectionPropertyData) ? obj : null);
			if (val == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE." + val.SelectedItem, "value"));
			if (text == null || VAVarFindUCaseIndex(list, text) == -1)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (VarType item3 in list2)
			{
				if (text == item3.UCase)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			ClearVarType();
		}

		private void ClearVarType()
		{
			((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndSet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE.@xVarName", "value", (object)"");
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue");
		}

		public List<VarType> VAGetVarIndex(List<VarType> VarTypes, ObjType oType)
		{
			List<VarType> list = new List<VarType>();
			switch (oType)
			{
			case ObjType.otAll:
				list = VarTypes;
				break;
			case ObjType.otBlock:
				foreach (VarType VarType in VarTypes)
				{
					if (VarType.UCase == "BLOCK-VAR" || VarType.UCase == "BLOCK-VEC" || VarType.UCase == "MEAS-VAR" || VarType.UCase == "CONT-VAR" || VarType.UCase == "CONFIG-VAR")
					{
						list.Add(VarType);
					}
				}
				break;
			case ObjType.otStream:
				foreach (VarType VarType2 in VarTypes)
				{
					if (VarType2.UCase == "STREAM-VAR" || VarType2.UCase == "MOLE-FLOW" || VarType2.UCase == "MOLE-FRAC" || VarType2.UCase == "MASS-FLOW" || VarType2.UCase == "MASS-FRAC" || VarType2.UCase == "STDVOL-FLOW" || VarType2.UCase == "STDVOL-FRAC" || VarType2.UCase == "STREAM-VEC" || VarType2.UCase == "STREAM-PROP" || VarType2.UCase == "SUBSTRM-VEC" || VarType2.UCase == "COMPATTR-VAR" || VarType2.UCase == "COMPATTR-VEC" || VarType2.UCase == "SUBSATTR-VAR" || VarType2.UCase == "SUBSATTR-VEC" || VarType2.UCase == "HEAT-DUTY" || VarType2.UCase == "WORK-POWER" || VarType2.UCase == "HEAT-TEMP" || VarType2.UCase == "HEAT-TEND" || VarType2.UCase == "QVEC" || VarType2.UCase == "TVEC" || VarType2.UCase == "STREAM-COST" || VarType2.UCase == "POWER-POWER")
					{
						list.Add(VarType2);
					}
				}
				break;
			case ObjType.otUtility:
				foreach (VarType VarType3 in VarTypes)
				{
					if (VarType3.UCase == "PARAMETER" || VarType3.UCase == "LOCAL-PARAM" || VarType3.UCase == "BALANCE-VAR" || VarType3.UCase == "PRESR-VAR" || VarType3.UCase == "UTILITY")
					{
						list.Add(VarType3);
					}
				}
				break;
			case ObjType.otProperty:
				foreach (VarType VarType4 in VarTypes)
				{
					if (VarType4.UCase == "UNARY-PARAM" || VarType4.UCase == "UNARY-COR-EL" || VarType4.UCase == "UN-COR-VEC" || VarType4.UCase == "BI-PARAM" || VarType4.UCase == "BI-COR-EL" || VarType4.UCase == "BI-COR-VEC" || VarType4.UCase == "NC-PARAM" || VarType4.UCase == "NC-COR-EL" || VarType4.UCase == "NC-COR-VEC" || VarType4.UCase == "GROUP-PARAM" || VarType4.UCase == "GROUP-BI-PAR" || VarType4.UCase == "GR-BI-COR-EL")
					{
						list.Add(VarType4);
					}
				}
				break;
			case ObjType.otReactions:
				foreach (VarType VarType5 in VarTypes)
				{
					if (VarType5.UCase == "CHEM-VAR" || VarType5.UCase == "REACT-VAR")
					{
						list.Add(VarType5);
					}
				}
				break;
			case ObjType.otCosting:
				foreach (VarType VarType6 in VarTypes)
				{
					if (VarType6.UCase == "BYPROD-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "CAPITAL-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "CBLOCK-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "LABOR-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "OPER-COST-VA" || VarType6.UCase == "PRODUCT-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "PROFIT-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "RAW-MAT-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "STARTUP-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "UNIT-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "UTILITY-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "WASTE-VAR" || VarType6.UCase == "WORK-CAP-VAR")
					{
						list.Add(VarType6);
					}
				}
				break;
			case ObjType.otOther:
				foreach (VarType VarType7 in VarTypes)
				{
					if (VarType7.UCase == "MEAS-VAR" || VarType7.UCase == "CONT-VAR")
					{
						list.Add(VarType7);
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			return list;
		}

		public int VAVarFindUCaseIndex(List<VarType> VarTypes, string UCase)
		{
			int result = -1;
			int num = 0;
			foreach (VarType VarType in VarTypes)
			{
				if (UCase == VarType.UCase)
				{
					result = num;
					break;
				}
				num++;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void Display2()
		{
			List<PromptType> list = VarAccessing.VAPromptInit();
			byte b = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < cmdFind_Visibilities.Length; i++)
			{
				cmdFind_Visibilities[i] = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_BlockVarVisible", (object)"0");
			((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_HybridVarVisible", (object)"0");
			for (int j = 0; j < list.Count; j++)
			{
				PromptType promptType = list[j];
				string text = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + promptType.Variable + ".@xInt", "can_enter"));
				if (text == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string text2 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + promptType.Variable + ".@xInt", "value"));
				if (text2 == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				bool flag = !(text == "1");
				if (flag && (!(promptType.Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Units) || !(text2 != "")))
				{
					continue;
				}
				_label_Visibilities[b] = Visibility.Visible;
				_label_captions[b] = list[j].Text;
				object obj = ((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input." + promptType.Variable + ".@xInt", "option_list");
				if (obj == null || obj.GetType() == typeof(DBNull))
				{
					_combo_Visibilities[b] = Visibility.Collapsed;
					_textEdit_Visibilities[b] = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				else
				{
					_combo_Visibilities[b] = Visibility.Visible;
					_textEdit_Visibilities[b] = Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				if (promptType.Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Variable && _combo_Visibilities[b] == Visibility.Visible)
				{
					cmdFind_Visibilities[b] = Visibility.Visible;
					ComboWidth[b] = 154;
				}
				else if (list[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Variable && (cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue == "Block-Var" || cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue == "Sensor-Var"))
				{
					_combo_Visibilities[b] = Visibility.Collapsed;
					_textEdit_Visibilities[b] = Visibility.Collapsed;
					string text3 = CxsConverter.ValueToString(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_BLOCK.@xInt", "value"));
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text3))
					{
						text3 = ((MMViewModel)this).GetFullBlockName("@main", text3);
						if (((MMViewModel)this).GetModelCategoryByBlockName(text3) == 14 || cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue == "Sensor-Var")
						{
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_HybridVarVisible", (object)"1");
						}
						else
						{
							((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("p_BlockVarVisible", (object)"1");
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					ComboWidth[b] = 182;
				}
				if (((MMViewModel)this).View is MMSensitivi_Input_Define mMSensitivi_Input_Define)
				{
					object obj2 = ((FrameworkElement)(object)mMSensitivi_Input_Define).FindName("txtRowsvw_" + b);
					MMTextEdit val = (MMTextEdit)((obj2 is MMTextEdit) ? obj2 : null);
					object obj3 = ((FrameworkElement)(object)mMSensitivi_Input_Define).FindName("cboRowsvw_" + b);
					MMComboEdit val2 = (MMComboEdit)((obj3 is MMComboEdit) ? obj3 : null);
					object obj4 = ((FrameworkElement)(object)mMSensitivi_Input_Define).FindName("txtRowsvw_" + b);
					MMValueEdit val3 = (MMValueEdit)((obj4 is MMValueEdit) ? obj4 : null);
					if (flag || list[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Sentence)
					{
						if (val != null)
						{
							((AesControlBase)val).IsMMEnabled = false;
						}
						if (val2 != null)
						{
							((AesControlBase)val2).IsMMEnabled = false;
						}
						if (val3 != null)
						{
							((AesControlBase)val3).IsMMEnabled = false;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						if (val != null)
						{
							((AesControlBase)val).IsMMEnabled = true;
						}
						if (val2 != null)
						{
							((AesControlBase)val2).IsMMEnabled = true;
						}
						if (val3 != null)
						{
							((AesControlBase)val3).IsMMEnabled = true;
						}
					}
					if (val2 != null)
					{
						if ((list[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Units || list[j].Text == Resources.PromptType_Text_Sentence) && !((AesControlBase)val2).IsMMEnabled)
						{
							((AesComboEdit)val2).DisplayAsTextBox = true;
						}
						else
						{
							((AesComboEdit)val2).DisplayAsTextBox = false;
						}
					}
				}
				if (list[j].ConvertToMixedCase)
				{
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("Rowsvw_" + b + "_IsMixedCase", (object)"1");
				}
				else
				{
					((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("Rowsvw_" + b + "_IsMixedCase", (object)"0");
				}
				((CxvViewModel)this).ResolveUpdateParameter("vwrow" + b, (object)promptType.Variable);
				b++;
				if (b > 7)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			for (int k = b; k <= 7; k++)
			{
				_label_Visibilities[k] = Visibility.Collapsed;
				_combo_Visibilities[k] = Visibility.Collapsed;
				_textEdit_Visibilities[k] = Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("Label_Captions");
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("Label_Visibilities");
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("Combo_Visibilities");
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("TextEdit_Visibilities");
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("cmdFind_Visibilities");
			((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("ComboWidth");
		}

		public static string StrConvMixCase(string InpVal)
		{
			string text = "";
			try
			{
				int result = 0;
				if (int.TryParse(InpVal, out result))
				{
					text = InpVal;
					return text;
				}
				string[] array = InpVal.Split('-');
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					if (array[i].Length > 1)
					{
						array[i] = array[i].Substring(0, 1).ToUpper() + array[i].Substring(1).ToLower();
					}
					else if (array[i].Length > 0)
					{
						array[i] = array[i].ToUpper();
					}
				}
				string[] array2 = array;
				foreach (string text2 in array2)
				{
					text = text + text2 + "-";
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return text;
		}

		public override bool PasteVariable(IEnumerable<string> paths)
		{
			if (paths != null && paths.Count() > 0)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				string[] oldIds = CxsConverter.ValueToStringArray(((CxvViewModel)this).ExpandDataPathAndGet("@main.Input.FVN_VARTYPE", "child_ids"));
				foreach (string path in paths)
				{
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
					{
						SmgeExtensions.SMVariableAccessingParse((MMViewModel)(object)this, path, (VarAcessType)0, ref flag);
					}
				}
				UpdateSelectedRow(oldIds);
				((CxvObservableObject)this).OnPropertyChanged("cboVarTypevw_SelectedValue");
				return true;
			}
			return ((MMViewModel)this).PasteVariable(paths);
		}

		{

			if (!flag)
			{
				DependencyObject ancestorFromType = Utilities.GetAncestorFromType(e.OriginalSource as DependencyObject, typeof(MMScrollArea), false);
				MMScrollArea val = (MMScrollArea)(object)((ancestorFromType is MMScrollArea) ? ancestorFromType : null);
				flag = val != null && string.Equals(((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Name, "grdDefine");
			}
			return flag;
		}
